# Wolf Pet
Aliases: Mystery Wolf Pet
Categories: Pets, Pages with auctionable items without valid auctionable item IDs, Craftable items, Templates with deprecated argument names, Spruce Log Collection, Combat pets
[Wolf Pet]
Type: [[Combat]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: COMMON ([[Rarity]])–LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Spruce Log III (3) ([[Spruce Log]])
Mob: Floating Head
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Spruce Log]] 144x (2 stacks plus 16 (144 in total)) [[Egg]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: WOLF
Tooltip: Combat Pet; Health: +0.5➡50; True Defense: +0.1➡10; Crit Damage: +0.1➡10%; Speed: +0.2➡20; Alpha Dog Take 0.1➡10% less damage from wolves.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Wolf Pet is a craftable [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]] that is unlocked in [[Spruce Log|Spruce Log III (3)]]. It is generally considered the best pet for leveling up [[Combat]] due to level 100 [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] versions of this pet giving 30 [[Combat Wisdom]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|5k [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Spruce Log]]|5 Minutes|

|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|25k [[Coins]]|16x [[Enchanted Spruce Log]]|15 Minutes|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]|32x [[Enchanted Spruce Log]]|30 Minutes|

## Tips
- The Wolf Pet is a great pet for those struggling to survive against [[Sven Packmaster]], although the slayer should be easy when using healing items and [[God Potion]], which leaves room for pets better for [[Magic Find]].
- Additionally, the Wolf Pet is useful for spawning [[Slayer]] bosses prior to obtaining their respective pets ( [[Ghoul Pet]], [[Tarantula Pet]], [[Hound Pet]], [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Enderman Pet]], and [[Wisp Pet]]).
  - The Wolf Pet grants additive [[Combat Wisdom]], whereas the aforementioned Slayer-themed pets grant a multiplicative [[Combat]] XP boost, making them superior to the Wolf Pet.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|500 [[Coins]]|128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Rotten Flesh]]|10 Seconds|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|2.5k [[Coins]]|256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Rotten Flesh]]|20 Seconds|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|5k [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Rotten Flesh]]|30 Seconds|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted Rotten Flesh]]|45 Seconds|

## Tips
- The Zombie Pet is mostly unviable outside dungeons. Although this pet is cheap even at [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it is severely outclassed by many other pets such as [[Ankylosaurus Pet]], [[Tiger Pet]], or even the [[Ghoul Pet]] as the additional [[Combat]] XP is more useful for progression and the bonuses to [[Vitality]] can increase survivability for more consistent [[Revenant Horror]] kills.
  - Keeping the pet at [[Rarity|EPIC]] is often better for the explicit usage of outside dungeons, as it would take less XP to level up and the lack of relevant Undead armor outside dungeons. Only upgrade if you intend to use the [[Revived Heart]] or [[Reaper Mask]] to compensate for being underlevelled and having insufficient [[Effective Health]].
- Inside dungeons, the pet is oftentimes a great pick for early floors. Most mobs in the Catacombs are [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]], and many of the other cheap combat pets are far less useful inside dungeons due to its unique environment. As most dungeons armor before dungeonised [[Unstable Dragon Armor]] or [[Adaptive Armor]] are Undead items, they all receive a significant buff from this pet.
  - Note that past Floor 4 it is recommended to use a more defensive pet such as [[Turtle Pet]] for more survivability or a utility pet such as [[Horse Pet|Horse]] or [[Skeleton Horse Pet]] for extra mobility, as damage is rarely the bottleneck and the bonuses provided by this pet is not as significant past early floors.
  - Archers should use the [[Skeleton Pet]] instead of Zombie due to its buffs to arrows.
- In both scenarios, the [[Wither Skeleton Pet]] can be a cheaper alternative as it is far easier to level up a [[Mining]] pet than a [[Combat]] pet. It also provides sufficient value even in Floor 7, as well as the damage over time effect often providing greater DPS against [[Revenant Horror]] bosses. It does not affect Tier V bosses, but by the time you challenge them you should be using a better pet instead, such as the aforementioned [[Ankylosaurus Pet]].
## Trivia
- The [[Reforging|Undead]] reforge does not classify an armor piece as "Undead", and therefore will not cause the stats of the armor to be increased by the Zombie Pet.
- The third ability of the Zombie Pet used to only buff [[Defense]] on the armor pieces.
- This used to be one of the worst pets in the game, as it was unlocked far later in the game and costed 8 [[Zombie's Heart|Zombie's Hearts]] to craft, with [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] not being guaranteed due to old pet crafting mechanics. Its perks were also far worse than its present perks.

# Ability Damage
Aliases: Ability Damage Calculation, Int Scaling
Categories: Player Statistics, Confirmations needed, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Pages with math equations, Mechanics
[Ability Damage]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases damage dealt by abilities.
Increasing: [[Enchanting]], [[Dark Goggles]] and their upgrades, [[Accessory Power]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E002
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Beacon ()
Ability Damage or Bonus Ability Damage is one of the player [[Stats]]. It increases the damage dealt by [[Damage Calculation|damage abilities]].
## Terminology
Ability Damage and Base Ability Damage are distinct concepts.
Ability Damage refers to the Bonus Ability Damage statistic which increases [[Damage Calculation|Final Damage]] when dealing [[Damage Calculation|Magic Damage]]
  Base Ability Damage is a value which every [[Damage Calculation/Damage Abilities|damage ability]] has. It cannot be increased and is also used to calculate [[Damage Calculation|Final Damage]] when dealing [[Damage Calculation|Magic Damage]].
## Mechanics
Bonus Ability Damage increases the damage dealt by [[Damage Calculation|damage abilities]].
## Sources
### Increasing Base value of Bonus Ability Damage
- [[Enchanting]] (0.5% per level)
- Increasing base [[Intelligence]] and [[Mana]]
### Pets
Certain [[Pets]] grant Ability Damage. The [[Pets|Pet]] must be equipped in order to take effect.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sheep Pet]]|[[Sheep Pet]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])-LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Alchemy Pet ([[Pets]])|+0.2 → +20|
|[[Crow Pet]]|[[Crow Pet]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])-LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Combat Pet ([[Pets]])|+0.2 → +20|
|[[Blaze Pet]]|[[Blaze Pet]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])-LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Combat Pet ([[Pets]])|Nether Embodiment; Increases Combat and Miscellaneous stats by 0.1% → 10% while on the [[Crimson Isle]].|
|[[Ender Dragon Pet]]|[[Ender Dragon Pet]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Combat Pet ([[Pets]])|Superior; Increases Combat stats and [[Magic Find]] by 0% → 10%.|
#### Pet Items
Certain [[Pet Items]] grant Ability Damage. The [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] must be equipped on the player's active [[Pets|Pet]] to take effect.

|-|-|-|-|-|

|[[Hephaestus Relic]]|[[Hephaestus Relic]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Increases all pet stats by 50%.|Recipe: [[Minos Relic]] ×3 + [[Hephaestus Anvil]] → [[Hephaestus Relic]]|
#### Accessory Powers
Certain [[Accessories]] grant Ability Damage. They must be either in the [[Player]]'s [[Accessory Bag]] or inventory to take effect.
|Icon|Item|Rarity|Ability Damage|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Beastslayer's Codex]]; [[Beastslayer's Codex]]; [[Beastslayer's Codex]]|[[Beastslayer's Codex]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])-MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|+1-3|Rarity Based on [[Bestiary]] Milestones|
Certain [[Powers|Accessory Powers]] grant Ability Damage. The [[Powers|Accessory Power]] must be selected at [[Maxwell]] in order to take effect.

|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ender Monocle]]|[[Ender Monocle]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Accessory Power ([[Powers]])|+3|
|[[Eccentric Painting]]|[[Eccentric Painting]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Accessory Power ([[Powers]])|+5|

Certain [[Weapons]] grant Ability Damage. They must be used or held to grant their effects.

Certain [[Armor]] pieces grant Ability Damage. The [[Armor]] must be equipped to take effect.

### Modifiers
Certain modifiers can be applied to items to grant Ability Damage. The items with the modifiers applied to must be used to take effect.
#### Reforges
Certain [[Reforging|Reforges]] can be applied to items via an [[Anvil]] or the [[Blacksmith]] to grant Ability Damage. The items with the [[Reforging|Reforge]] applied must be used to take effect.

### Maximizing Ability Damage
- [[Candidate Jerry]] - 10% Ability Damage
- [[Enchanting|Enchanting LX (60)]] - 30 Ability Damage
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Sheep Pet]] or [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Crow Pet]] [[Lv100] + [[Hephaestus Relic]]] - 30 Ability Damage
- [[Daedalus Blade]] enchanted with [[Enchanted Book|Chimera V]] - 60 Ability Damage
  - [[Chimera|Chimera V]]: Copies 100% of the pet's stats - 30 Ability Damage
  - [[Daedalus Blade]]: Copies 100% of the pet's Ability Damage's stats - 30 Ability Damage
- [[Wither Goggles]] [ + [[Dragon Horn|Renowned]]] - 49.5 & +1% Ability Damage
- [[Storm's Armor|Storm's Chestplate]] [ [[Red Scarf|Loving]]] - 5 Ability Damage
- [[Storm's Armor|Storm's Leggings]] [ [[Dragon Horn|Renowned]]] - +1% Ability Damage
- [[Storm's Armor|Storm's Boots]] [ [[Dragon Horn|Renowned]]] - +1% Ability Damage
- [[Beacon|Beacon V]] [ [[Scorched Power Crystal]]] - 6 Ability Damage
- [[Eccentric Painting|Bizarre Power]] - 5 Ability Damage
*While the [[Daedalus Blade]] + [[Chimera]] combo provides the most ability damage possible, this is mostly pointless as the weapon doesn't have a magic ability.
## Trivia
- Some abilities deal Magic Damage that ignore the target's [[Armor]] stat.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- The [[Sheep Pet]] and [[Crow Pet]] are the only pets that give a specific amount of Ability Damage.
  - However, some pets that give % bonuses to stats, such as the [[Blaze Pet]], can increase it with their abilities.

# Alchemy Wisdom
Categories: Player Statistics
[Alchemy Wisdom]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the Alchemy XP gain.
Increasing: Alchemy XP Boost Potion ([[Potions]]), [[Booster Cookie]], and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Symbol: ☯
Unicode Code Point: U+262F
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Book and Quill ()
Alchemy Wisdom is one of the player's [[Stats|wisdom stats]]. It increases the amount of [[Alchemy|Alchemy XP]] the player gains.
## Mechanics
[[Alchemy|Alchemy XP]] gained by the player is multiplied by 1+AlchemyWisdom100. For example, if a player with 20 Alchemy Wisdom gained +100 [[Alchemy|Alchemy XP]], they would gain 1.2x that amount, or +120 [[Alchemy|Alchemy XP]].

# Block Drop Calculation
Categories: Player Statistics, Pages with math equations
With the release of the v0.11 Dwarven Mines update, most of the block drop increases have been condensed into multiple fortune stats for each relevant activity ([[Mining Fortune]], [[Farming Fortune]], [[Foraging Fortune]]), greatly simplifying how the drop chances are calculated.
## Mining
Average Amount=Base Amount×Base Drop Multiplier×(1+Mining Fortune100)+Additive Bonus
Where:
- Base Amount is the amount of drops received without any bonuses.
- Base Drop Multiplier includes bonuses that increase multiplicatively with one another ([[Mining Fiesta]], SkyMall's 5x Titanium).
- Mining Fortune is your total [[Mining Fortune]] bonus, including ore-specific bonuses.
- Additive Bonus is comprised of bonuses that are unaffected by the base drop amount or by [[Mining Fortune]](e.g. [[Fallen Stars|Fallen Star]], [[Reforging|Mithraic]] reforge).
### Example
Mining Titanium Ore with a Mining Fiesta and 5x Titanium active while near a Fallen Star, with 300 [[Mining Fortune]].
2×2×5×(1+300100)+2=82
## Farming
Average Amount=Base Amount×(1+Farming Fortune100)
For a list of modifiers that affect crop drops, see [[Farming Fortune]].
## Foraging
Average Amount=Base Amount×(1+Foraging Fortune100)
For a list of modifiers that affect log drops, see [[Foraging Fortune]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Block drop calculation undergone massive updates on release of [[Dwarven Mines]].|

# Block Fortune
Categories: Player Statistics
[Block Fortune]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the amount of drops from [[Blocks]]
Increasing: [[Heart of the Mountain]], [[Armor]], Reforges ([[Reforging]]), [[Attributes]], [[Consumables]], [[Snail Pet]]
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E053
Block Fortune is a [[Stats|gathering stat]] that increases drops from [[Blocks]]. It has no effect while on a [[Private Island]].
## Mechanics
Every 100 Block Fortune a player has increases the amount of drops received from [[Blocks]] by 100%. Any excess Block Fortune increases the percentage chance of receiving further drops.
For example, if a player has 130 Block Fortune, they are guaranteed to receive 2x drops, with a 30% chance to receive 3x drops instead.
## Increasing Base Block Fortune
Players have a base 0 Block Fortune. It can be increased by the following permanent sources:
|Slot|Name|Type|Bonus|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dwarven O's Block Bran]]|[[Dwarven O's Block Bran]]|Consumable Item|2 per consumption up to 10 Block Fortune|

# Bonus Pest Chance
Aliases: BPC, Pest Chance, Pest chance
Categories: Player Statistics
[Bonus Pest Chance]
Type: Stat
Uses: Chance to increase the amount of Pests that spawn.
Increasing: [[Pesthunter Badge]], [[Sprayonator]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E019
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Dirt ()
Bonus Pest Chance is a [[Stats|Stat]] that increases the chance for multiple [[Pests]] to spawn at once. Each multiple of 100 is a guaranteed additional Pest spawn, and the remaining is a percentage chance for one more.
## Mechanics
With 0 Bonus Pest Chance, when 4 or more Pests are on [[The Garden]] at once, the player's [[Farming Fortune]] (as well as each crop-specific fortune stat) is reduced, first by 5% and then multiples of 15%.
- [[Farming Fortune]] will continue to be lost for each additional Pest, up to 8, after which no more Pests will spawn until at least one is killed.
- The maximum [[Farming Fortune]] loss is 75%.
- For each 100 Bonus Pest Chance the player has, they can have 1 more Pest on their Garden before their [[Farming Fortune]] starts being reduced. The reduction then starts at 5% and scales from there.
Effect of number of [[Pests]] on [[Farming Fortune]]:
|Pests|[[Farming Fortune]] Reduction 0-99|[[Farming Fortune]] Reduction 100-199|[[Farming Fortune]] Reduction 200-299|[[Farming Fortune]] Reduction 300-399|[[Farming Fortune]] Reduction 400-499|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|0||||||
|1||||||
|2||||||
|3||||||
|4|5%|||||
|5|15%|5%||||
|6|30%|15%|5%|||
|7|50%|30%|15%|5%||
|8|75%|50%|30%|15%|5%|

## Tips
- The [[Pesthunter Badge]] and its upgraded versions can toggle Bonus Pest Chance, therefore there are no drawbacks to permanent gains in Bonus Pest Chance, such as the [[Wriggling Larva]].
- [[Pests|Pest]] spawns can be reduced with the use of [[Pest Repellent]] or [[Pest Repellent MAX]], increasing the cooldown of [[Pests]] spawning by 2x and 4x respectively.
## Notes
- When disabling Bonus Pest Chance using [[Pesthunter Badge]], the pests that would not reduce the player's [[Farming Fortune]], thanks to the added bonus of every 100 , will now reduce the [[Farming Fortune]].
## Trivia
- It is the only [[Stats|Farming Stat]] that isn't disabled by default on the player's [[Private Island]].
  - Comparatively, [[Timber]] is the only [[Stats|Foraging Stat]] that isn't disabled by default on the player's [[Private Island]].
- This and [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] are the only stat that can be disabled by the player, which can be done using [[Pesthunter Badge]] or its upgrades.
  - [[Lava Shell Necklace]] sets the player's [[Mending]] and [[Health Regen]] to 0, without disabling them.
  - [[Treasure Hook]] nullifies the effect of [[Sea Creature Chance]], [[Double Hook Chance]] and [[Trophy Chance]], [[Tera Shell Necklace]] negates the effects [[Trophy Chance]] and [[Bronze Hunter Armor]] and its upgrades cancel [[Sea Creature Chance]] without affecting the stats themselves.
- Unlike all other stats with Chance in their name, Bonus Pest Chance, [[Crit Chance]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]] have no cap.

# Breaking Power
Aliases: Mining Power
Categories: Player Statistics, Confirmations needed, Mechanics, Pickaxes
[Breaking Power]
Type: Stat
Uses: Allows you to mine stronger blocks.
Increasing: [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]], [[Pocket Iceberg]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E005
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Emerald ()
Breaking Power is a [[Stats|mining stat]], that determines which blocks can be broken.
## Mechanics
Breaking Power affects which blocks the player is allowed to break, depending on said block's strength.
- Breaking Power is not active in areas without the custom mining systems, such as the [[Private Island]], the [[Hub Island|Hub]], the [[The Farming Islands (disambiguation)|Farming Islands]] (i.e. [[The Barn]] and [[Mushroom Desert]]), [[The Park]], [[The Garden]] and [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
- Breaking Power also increases the [[Damage Calculation|Final Damage]] inflicted to [[Glacial (Mob Type)|Glacial]] mobs. Every 1 Breaking Power is 150x more potent than 1 [[Damage]].

[Σnon permanent and unconditional= 1]
[∑max= 12]
## Modifiers
Certain Modifiers can be applied to items to grant Breaking Power. The items with modifiers applied must be used to take effect.
### Heart of the Mountain
- The [[Heart of the Mountain|Maniac Miner]] ability grants 1 Breaking Power and a stack of 5-15 [[Mining Fortune]] per block broken for 25-35 seconds. Each block broken consumes 20 [[Mana]], including those broken through [[Mining Spread]] and [[Gemstone Spread]]
  - The [[Ultimate Wise]] enchantment decreases the [[Mana]] cost.
  - This ability is currently bugged.
## Bugs
- With an active [[Heart of the Mountain|Maniac Miner]] ability and using a [[Tools|Mining Tool]] that would otherwise have a Breaking Power too low to break a given block, [[Gemstone Spread]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; Not sure for gemstone spread; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] and [[Mining Spread]] do not apply.

# Carpentry Wisdom
Categories: Player Statistics
[Carpentry Wisdom]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the Carpentry XP gain.
Increasing: [[Booster Cookie]], and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Symbol: ☯
Unicode Code Point: U+262F
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Book and Quill ()
Carpentry Wisdom is one of the player's [[Stats|wisdom stats]]. It increases the amount of [[Carpentry|Carpentry XP]] the player gains.
## Mechanics
[[Carpentry|Carpentry XP]] gained by the player is multiplied by 1+CarpentryWisdom100. For example, if a player with 20 Carpentry Wisdom gained +100 [[Carpentry|Carpentry XP]], they would gain 1.2x that amount, or +120 [[Carpentry|Carpentry XP]].
## Increasing Base Carpentry Wisdom
|Name|Remarks||
|-|-|-|
|[[Paint Drying Simulator]]|Can be watched a maximum of 7 times.|+0 → +3.5|
## Increasing Bonus Carpentry Wisdom
### Consumables
Certain items can be consumed to grant Carpentry Wisdom.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Booster Cookie]]|[[Booster Cookie]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Consumable|+25|
|[[Celestial Mason Jar]]|[[Celestial Mason Jar]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Consumable|+3|
### Maximizing Carpentry Wisdom
- [[Booster Cookie]] (+25 Carpentry Wisdom)
- [[Quantum|Quantum V]] (+2 Carpentry Wisdom)
- All paint in [[Paint Drying Simulator]] watched. (+3.5 Carpentry Wisdom)
- [[Celestial Mason Jar]](+3 Carpentry Wisdom)
- Completing [[Refractive Paint Palette]]
## Notes
- Despite Mayor [[Derpy]] granting a 50% bonus to all [[Skills|Skill]] experience, they do not affect wisdom stats.
  - Events granting Skill Experience boost are multiplicative with the "MOAR SKILLZ!!!" perk, and also don't affect Carpentry Wisdom.

# Charm Chance
Aliases: Charmed, Charming
Categories: Player Statistics, Confirmations needed
[Charm Chance]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the chance of capturing the Attribute Shard ([[Attributes]]) of certain mobs.
Increasing: [[Lushlilac]], [[Oceandy]], [[Hunting]], Charmed ([[Attributes]])
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: 100
[Symbol]
Symbol: ❣
Unicode Code Point: U+2763
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): [[Lushlilac]]'s old texture ()
Charm Chance is a [[Hunting]] stat. It increases the chance of capturing the [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]] of certain [[Mob|Mobs]] when they are killed.
## Charming Mechanics
If a player has any Charm Chance effect active, they can capture the [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]] of certain [[Mob|Mobs]] upon death. The amount of shards gained is dependent on the player's [[Hunting Fortune]].
[[Mob|Mobs]] cannot be charmed if a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] is used to hunt them. However, charmable [[Mob|Mobs]] hunted with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] or [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] still can be charmed.
The Charm Chance can be increased through various ways:
|Charm Source|Charm Chance|Affected Mobs|Chance Modifiers|
|-|-|-|-|
|Hunting I-L ([[Hunting]])|0.04%-2%|All||
|Charmed I-X ([[Attributes]])|0.05%-0.5%|All||
|[[Lushlilac]]|0.5%|All|Tasty Forest Essence Shop Perk ([[Forest Essence]]); (1.1x)|
|[[Lushlilac Bonbon]]|0.625%|All|Tasty Forest Essence Shop Perk ([[Forest Essence]]); (1.1x)|
|[[Prime Lushlilac Bonbon]]|0.75%|All|Tasty Forest Essence Shop Perk ([[Forest Essence]]); (1.1x)|
|[[Exalted Lushlilac Bonbon]]|1%|All|Tasty Forest Essence Shop Perk ([[Forest Essence]]); (1.1x)|
|[[Oceandy]]|1.25%|All [[Sea Creatures]]|Tasty Forest Essence Shop Perk ([[Forest Essence]]); (1.1x)|
|[[Brine Tonic]]|2.5%|[[Sea Creatures]] on [[Moonglade Marsh]] and [[Torrhus Canyon]]|Tasty Forest Essence Shop Perk ([[Forest Essence]]); (1.1x)|
|[[Candycomb]]|1.25%|All Dungeon Mobs ([[Catacombs Mobs]])|Tasty Forest Essence Shop Perk ([[Forest Essence]]); (1.1x)|
When killing an [[Elusive]] mob that can drop a shard, Charm Chance is ignored and the [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]] is guaranteed to drop.

# Cold
Categories: Player Statistics
[Cold]
Type: Stat
Uses: Lowers player's [[Mining Speed]], [[Speed]] and kills them once reaching -100 Cold
Decreasing: Being in [[Glacite Tunnels]], [[Glacite Mineshafts]], the Icy Biome of the [[Critter Safari]] and being attacked in the [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Min Value: -100
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E006
Cold is a player mechanic in the [[Glacite Tunnels]], [[Glacite Mineshafts]] and the Icy Biome of the [[Critter Safari]], that lowers [[Mining Speed]] and [[Speed]].
## Mechanics
On the opposite of [[Heat]] and unlike all other [[Stats]], Cold evolves below 0.
### Effects of Cold
- Every -1 Cold the player has below the opposite of their [[Cold Resistance]] reduces their [[Mining Speed]] by 0.5%.
- Every -25 Cold the player has regardless of [[Cold Resistance]] reduces their [[Speed]] cap by 25%.
- At -100 Cold, the player dies instantly.
- Every -1 grants +0.5 → 2 [[Mining Fortune]], using a [[Tools|Mining Tool]] reforged to [[Reforging|Glacial]] ( [[Frigid Husk]]).
- Every -1 grants +2 [[Mining Fortune]] while the [[Fortunate Freezing]] event is ongoing.
### Decreasing Cold
Cold decreases in [[Glacite Tunnels]], [[Glacite Mineshafts]] and the Icy Biome of the [[Critter Safari]] only.
- Cold accumulates at a rate of -1 every x seconds:
  - x=5×1+Cold Resistance1+Cold Vulnerability
  [[Cold Resistance]]: +0.01, Source: For every 1 [[Cold Resistance]].; [[Cold Resistance]]: Cold Vulnerability, Source: Condition; [[Cold Resistance]]: +3.00, Source: Water ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]) in the Icy Biome of the [[Critter Safari]] or in the [[Great Glacite Lake]].; [[Cold Resistance]]: +0.25, Source: [[Glacite Mineshafts]], every 150 seconds, triggering on entry. This is accompanied by the music looping and a game message:; BRRR! It's getting colder in here...
- Cold can also be decreased by a flat amount from the following mobs. This can be mitigated by the [[Mammoth Pet]]'s Wooly Coat perk with a 1% → 100% chance.
  - 1 per attack from [[Glacite Bowman|Glacite Bowmen]], [[Glacite Caver|Glacite Cavers]], [[Glacite Mage|Glacite Mages]], [[Glacite Mutt|Glacite Mutts]]
  - 3 per attack from [[Littlefoot|Littlefeet]].
### Increasing Cold
Cold increases everywhere else.
- Cold depletes at a rate of +1 every 1 second, unaffected by [[Cold Resistance]].
- Cold can also be increased by a flat amount from the following:
  Source: [[Portable Campfire]], Cold: +50 . Cooldown of 5 minutes., Condition: [[Glacite Tunnels]], including [[Glacite Mineshafts]].; Source: [[Tungsten Regulator]], Type of source: Drill Part ([[Drill Parts]]), Cold: +1 / 100 Fuel ([[Machine Fuel]]) consumed on the Drill ([[Drills]]).; Source: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Penguin Pet]]'s Thick Blubber, Type of source: Pet ([[Pets]]) perk, Cold: +1 → +6 / Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) caught.; Source: [[Dwarven Base Camp]], Type of source: Location, Cold: Set to 0; Source: Changing Worlds, Type of source: Location, Cold: Set to 0; Source: "Dying" in the [[Critter Safari]], Cold: Set to 0

|-|-|

|Reaching -25 Cold|BRRR! It's getting really cold in here! But you've got to keep moving...|
|Reaching -50 Cold|BRRR! It's so cold that you can barely feel your fingers. Moving is getting difficult...|
|Reaching -75 Cold|BRRR! Your movement slows to a crawl as the cold threatens to take over. Time to get out of here...|
|Reaching -75 Cold in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]]|BRRR! Your movement slows to a crawl as the cold threatens to take over. Time to get out of here...|
|Reaching -75 Cold in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]]|The following title also appears:FROSTBITE! Find warmth before you freeze to death...|
|Reaching -90 Cold|BRRR! You're freezing! All you can think about is getting out of here to a warm campfire...|
|Reaching -90 Cold|The following title also appears:FROSTBITE! Find warmth before you freeze to death...|
|Reaching -100 Cold and dying|You froze to death.|
|Reaching -100 Cold and dying, in [[Glacite Mineshafts]]|You froze to death.; Maybe next time you'll find a way to escape the cold...|
|Reaching -100 Cold and dying, in the Icy biome of the [[Critter Safari]]|You fainted and lost some of your items!|
## Trivia
- The [[Phoenix Pet]]'s Rekindle perk saves the player from dying to -100 Cold.
  - When this happens, the player gets teleported back to the [[Dwarven Mines]] spawn point without the death penalty.

# Cold Resistance
Categories: Player Statistics
[Cold Resistance]
Type: Stat
Uses: Reduces how fast you gain [[Cold]] and weakens the detrimental effect of Cold to [[Mining Speed]].
Increasing: [[Pets]], [[Heart of the Mountain]], [[Ice Cold]], [[Cold Resistance Potion]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E006
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Ice ()
Cold Resistance is one of the player's [[Stats|Misc Stats]]. It reduces how fast the player gains [[Cold]], and also reduces the Cold's detrimental effect to [[Mining Speed]].
## Mechanics
### Slowing [[Cold]] Gain
For every 1 Cold Resistance, reduce how fast the player gains [[Cold]] by 1%, which stacks additively, meaning with 100 it will build up twice as slow.
### Numbing [[Cold]]'s Effects
With 0 Cold Resistance, [[Cold]] reduces the player's [[Mining Speed]] by 0.5% per Cold.
If the player's Cold Resistance is greater than their [[Cold]], this effect is completely negated.
Otherwise, the [[Mining Speed]] reduction is determined by the following formula:
Mining Speed Reduction=(Current Cold−Cold Resistance)/2
for example with 70 [[Cold]] and 55 Cold Resistance the [[Mining Speed]] is reduced by 7.5%.
7.5=(70−55)/2
## Increasing Base Cold Resistance

|[[Reaper Pepper]]|[[Reaper Pepper]]|Consumable Item|1 per consumption up to 5 Cold Resistance||
|[[Ice]]|Frozen Skin|Essence Shop Perk Ice Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]])|1–5|1–5|

### Theoretical Maximum Cold Resistance
- 5 [[Reaper Pepper]] (5 )
- [[Essence Shops|Ice Essence Shop]]
  - Frozen Skin perk (5 )
- [[Heart of the Mountain]]
  - Warm Heart perk (20 )
- Any Armor Set:
  - [[Ice Cold|Ice Cold V]] (20 )
- [[Pendant of Divan]] (10 )
- [[Safari Belt]]
  - Icy Biome Milestone 10 (10 )
- [[Dwarven Handwarmers]] (5 )
- [[Penguin Pet]]
  - At [Lv100] (15 )
  - [[Hephaestus Relic]] (7.5 )
  - [[Chimera|Chimera V]] (22.5 )
  - 10 Nearby Players (10 )
- [[Fried Frozen Chicken]] (1 )
- [[Organ Donor Artifact]] (1.5 )
- [[Wumpa Shard]] (5 )
- [[Cold Resistance Potion|Cold Resistance IV]] (10 )
- [[Will-o'-wisp]] (10 )
- [[Century Cakes|Corpse-Flavored Century Cake]] (1 )
- [[Candidate Jerry]] (10% )
With all of the above, players can reach 156.5 Cold Resistance or 174.35 Cold Resistance when Jerry is the active mayor. This will make them immune to [[Mining Speed]] reducing effect of [[Cold]].
[[Glacite Mineshafts]] only:
- [[Essence Shops|Fossil Essence Shop]]
  - Cut Loose perk
    - 10 [[Glacial (Mob Type)|Glacial]] mobs killed (25 )
- [[Heart of the Mountain]]
  - Mineshaft Mayhem perk (10 )
With all of the above, players can reach 191.5 Cold Resistance while inside [[Glacite Mineshafts]] or 210.65 Cold Resistance when Jerry is the active mayor.
[[Critter Safari]] only:
- [[Essence Shops|Safari Essence Shop]]
  - Thawing perk (25 )
- [[Safari Belt]]
  - Icy Biome Milestone 10 (10 )
With the above, players can reach 35 Cold Resistance while in the [[Critter Safari]].

# Crit Chance
Aliases: Critical Chance
Categories: Player Statistics, Crit Chance, Mechanics
[Crit Chance]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increasing the chance to deal a Critical Hit.
Increasing: [[Armor]], [[Enchantments]], Reforges ([[Reforging]]), [[Accessory Power]], [[Pets]], [[Combat]], and more
[Values]
Base Value: 30
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E02C
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Player Skull ()
Crit Chance (short for Critical Chance; often abbreviated CC) is one of the 14 Player Combat [[Stats]]. It dictates the odds of landing a Critical Hit.
## Mechanics
Crit Chance corresponds to the percentage chance that a melee or ranged hit will be a critical hits. When a critical hit lands, the [[Damage Calculation|damage indicator]] displayed will change appearance from gray to red, orange, yellow, and white colors instead. Crit Chance above 100% is normally not shown in-game, but nevertheless takes effect (for [[Overload]] etc.). It can be viewed in the stats breakdown in the SkyBlock Menu as the "Flat" value, as well as in the Hypixel API. Note: Crit Chance functions differently to vanilla Minecraft, meaning jumping has no effect.
### Damage Calculation
Critical hits deal increased [[Damage Calculation|Final Damage]] based on the [[Crit Damage]] stat.
## Increasing Base Crit Chance
Base Crit Chance is the Crit Chance without any potions, armor or weapons.
Players have a base Crit Chance of 30% by default. It can be increased by:
- Leveling the [[Combat]] Skill. (+0.5% per level)
Max base Crit Chance is 60%.

# Crop Fortune
Aliases: Cactus Fortune, Carrot Fortune, Cocoa Beans Fortune, Melon Fortune, Melon Slice Fortune, Moonflower Fortune, Mushroom Fortune, Nether Wart Fortune, Potato Fortune, Pumpkin Fortune, Sugar Cane Fortune, Sunflower Fortune, Wheat Fortune, Wild Rose Fortune
Categories: Player Statistics, Outdated pages, Farming, Garden
[Crop Fortune]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the amount of drops from a particular type of crop.
Increasing: [[Farming Tools]], Anita's shop ([[Anita]]), [[Carrolyn]], [[Pets]] and more.
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E051
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Corresponding crop item (e.g , )
Crop Fortune refers to a group of [[Stats]], such as [[Crop Fortune|Wheat Fortune]] or [[Crop Fortune|Carrot Fortune]], which increase a player's chance to drop more items from the corresponding [[Crops (disambiguation)|crop]]. Just like [[Farming Fortune]], it has no effect while on one's [[Private Island]].
## Mechanics
Crop Fortune increases the chance to drop bonus crop items from breaking a particular [[Crops (disambiguation)|crop]], similarly to how [[Farming Fortune]] increases the drop bonus crop items from any crop.
Each point of Crop Fortune or [[Farming Fortune]] translates to a 1% chance to get 100% more crop items.
Additionally, for every 100 points of Crop Fortune, the player is guaranteed to drop 100% bonus crop items.
For example, if the player has +233 [[Crop Fortune|Cactus Fortune]], they gain 300% drops and a 33% chance for 400% drops while breaking [[Cactus]].
To determine how many bonus items the player will get, their [[Farming Fortune]] is first added to their Crop Fortune stat corresponding to the crop they are breaking.

# Crop Growth
Aliases: Growth Speed
Categories: Player Statistics
Crop Growth is a mechanic that influences how fast crops planted in [[The Garden]] grow. 1 Crop Growth is equal to 1% growth speed.
## Crop Growing Mechanics
Every 10 ticks, 0.5s seconds, the server sends a number of random ticks to randomly selected crops within a plot. If a crop receives a random tick, it grows by one growth stage. If a crop is already fully grown, nothing happens. Due to this behavior, harvested crops replenish fastest when no crops are yet fully grown, and slowest when almost all crops are fully grown.
Crops only grow if the player is in [[The Garden]], regardless of distance from a plot. They do not grow if the player is on another island or offline.
Crops in [[The Garden]] do not require light to grow at maximum speed, but they do require water. Crops will not grow at all on completely dry soil, even with [[Bone Meal]] or [[Enchanted Bone Meal]], and receive a 40% growth speed debuff on soil that is not fully moist.
[[Pumpkin|Pumpkins]] and [[Melon|Melons]] only require water for the stem to grow. After being grown, new [[Pumpkin|Pumpkins]] and [[Melon|Melons]] grow regardless of soil moisture.
### Greenhouse
Crop Growth also affects Growth Stage timer in the Greenhouse.
## Increasing Crop Growth
|Source|Type|Crop Growth|
|-|-|-|
|Garden Level ([[The Garden]])|Garden ([[The Garden]])|10 per level after Garden I (1) ([[The Garden]]), up to +140|
|DNA Analysis Milestone ([[Crop Analyzer]])||10 per Milestone, up to +60|
|[[Locust Shard]] (Crop Speed ([[Attributes]]))|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|1 per Attribute level, up to +10|
At the maximum value of 210 Crop Growth, crops grow 210% faster.

# Crux Fortune
Categories: Player Statistics
[Crux Fortune]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the chance to drop extra Cruxes ([[Crux (disambiguation)]]) when killing Crux mobs
Increasing: [[Crux Talisman]] line of Accesories ([[Accessories]])
[Values]
Base Value: 0
[Symbol]
Symbol: ☘
Unicode Code Point: 2618
Crux Fortune is a metric that can be interpreted as a [[Stats|Rift Stat]] which increases the chance of receiving bonus drops from [[Crux Mobs]] ( [[Shy|Shies]], [[Shadow|Shadows]], [[Volt|Volts]], [[Scribe|Scribes]], [[Frozille|Frozilles]], [[Splatter|Splatters]] and [[Puff|Puffs]]).
## Mechanics
Crux Fortune increases the chance to drop bonus [[Crux (disambiguation)|Cruxes]] when killing Crux mobs in the [[Rift Dimension]]. Every 5 Crux Fortune corresponds to a 5% chance to drop an extra [[Crux (disambiguation)|Crux]]. Additionally, for every 100 Crux Fortune, the player is guaranteed to drop another extra [[Crux (disambiguation)|Crux]] on every kill.
For example, if a player has 115 Crux Fortune, they have a 100% chance of dropping 2 [[Crux (disambiguation)|cruxes]] and a 15% chance of dropping a third.
## Increasing Crux Fortune
To increase their Crux Fortune, a player must possess a [[Crux Talisman]] or one of its upgraded forms.
Each [[Crux Mobs|Crux Mob]] has its specific set of 4 kill milestones, numbered with Roman numerals Ⅰ-Ⅳ. Milestone Ⅰ is awarded at 10 kills of a specific mob, Milestone Ⅱ at 25, and Milestones Ⅲ and Ⅳ at 50 and 100 respectively. The Crux accessory awards +5 [[Rift Time]] and +5 Crux Fortune for each kill milestone met. This is the only way to increase Crux Fortune.
The maximum 140 Crux Fortune obtainable can be achieved through using a [[Celestial Starstone]], having killed 100 or more of [[Crux Mobs]].
## Trivia
- Crux Fortune does not appear in the [[Stats|Rift Stats]] menu.
## History

|May 4, 2023|Added Crux Fortune.|

# Damage
Aliases: Critical Hit, Ghost Damage, Physical Damage, Sword Swapping, Sword-Swapping, Swordswapping
Categories: Player Statistics, Article stubs, Mechanics
[Damage]
Type: Stat
Uses: Determines damage dealt
Increasing: [[Weapons]], [[Hot Potato Book]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E050
Damage is a non-displayed player statistic which is mostly given by the held [[Weapons|weapon]] (cf. Damage: +100 [[Aspect of the End]] ).
## Terminology
Damage and [[Damage Calculation|Final Damage]] are distinct concepts.
Damage refers to the non-displayed player statistic which is mostly given by the held [[Weapons|weapon]] (cf. Damage: +100 [[Aspect of the End]] ).
  Damage is a statistic used to calculate [[Damage Calculation|Final Damage]].
[[Damage Calculation|Final Damage]] is not a player statistic.
  It is the damage a player applies on enemy [[Health]] by damaging it.
  [[Damage Calculation|Final Damage]] is commonly confused with the Damage statistic as it is also called damage.
## Mechanics
[[Damage Calculation|Final Damage]] is increased by many factors such as Damage, [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]], [[Combat]] Ability Warrior and many more.

|Name|Stat|

## Theoretical Maximum
#### Non-Boostable Sources
These are sources that won't increase Double Hook Chance with the use of [[Bobbin' Time|Bobbin' Time V]].
- Fishing in a [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] that actively grants Double Hook Chance (+2 )
  - Also utilizing a [[Hotspot Sinker]] applied on the rod (+2 )
  - Additionally, fishing in a hotspot while equipped with a [[Tiki Mask]] (+2 )
    - The theoretical max [[Sea Creature Chance]] requires the player to use a full set of [[Magma Lord Armor]]. This contradicts the use of [[Tiki Mask]], and when swapping the two items, the theoretical max lowers by 0.9 [[Sea Creature Chance]]
#### Boostable Sources
These are sources that will increase Double Hook Chance with the use of [[Bobbin' Time|Bobbin' Time V]] (+20% )
- [[Giant Fishing Rod]] with [[Titan Line]] (+12 )
  - The theoretical max [[Sea Creature Chance]] requires the player to use a [[Hellfire Rod]]. This contradicts the use of [[Giant Fishing Rod]], and when swapping the two items, the theoretical max lowers by 16.8 [[Sea Creature Chance]]
- Activating either [[Titanoboa Shard]], [[Nessie Shard]], or [[Lord Jawbus Shard]] (+5 )
- Using a pet equipped with [[Burnt Texts]] (+5 )
  - The theoretical max [[Sea Creature Chance]] requires the player to have an active [[Megalodon Pet]] equipped with a [[Hephaestus Souvenir]]. This contradicts the use of [[Burnt Texts]], and when swapping the two items, the theoretical max lowers by 20 [[Sea Creature Chance]]
- [[Marina]]'s Double Trouble perk, with a theoretical max of 166.68 [[Sea Creature Chance]] (+16.668 )
A total of +52.4016% Double Hook Chance can be achieved.
## Trivia
- The effects of Double Hook Chance can be nullified using [[Treasure Hook]], which also negates the effects of [[Trophy Chance]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]]. Interestingly, it doesn't affect the amount of the stat in any way.
  - The effects of [[Trophy Chance]] are also negated via [[Tera Shell Necklace]].
  - Similarly, [[Lava Shell Necklace]] sets the player's [[Mending]] and [[Health Regen]] to 0, without disabling them.
  - [[Bonus Pest Chance]] can be disabled by the player using [[Pesthunter Badge]] and its upgrades.

# Dwarven Metal Fortune
Categories: Player Statistics
[Dwarven Metal Fortune]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the amount of drops from [[Dwarven Metals]]
Increasing: [[Heart of the Mountain]], [[Dwarven O's Metallic Minis]], [[Dwarven Metal Talisman]], Dwarven Expertise perk
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: 150
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E053
Dwarven Metal Fortune is a Gathering [[Stats|stat]] that increase drops from [[Dwarven Metals]].
## Mechanics
Every 100 Dwarven Metal Fortune a player has increases the amount of drops received from [[Dwarven Metals]] by 100%. Any excess Dwarven Metal Fortune increases the percentage chance of receiving further drops.
For example, if a player has a maximum of 150 Dwarven Metal Fortune, they are guaranteed to receive 2x drops, with a 50% chance to receive 3x drops instead.

## Increasing Base Dwarven Metal Fortune
Players have a base 0 Dwarven Metal Fortune. There is no way to increase it.

# Enchanting Wisdom
Categories: Player Statistics
[Enchanting Wisdom]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the Enchanting XP gain.
Increasing: Enchanting XP Boost Potion ([[Potions]]), [[Booster Cookie]], [[Pets]], and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Symbol: ☯
Unicode Code Point: U+262F
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Book and Quill ()
Enchanting Wisdom is one of the player's [[Wisdom]] stats. It increases the amount of [[Enchanting|Enchanting XP]] the player gains.
## Mechanics
[[Enchanting|Enchanting XP]] gained by the player is multiplied by 1+EnchantingWisdom100. For example, if a player with 20 Enchanting Wisdom gained +100 [[Enchanting|Enchanting XP]], they would gain 1.2x that amount, or +120 [[Enchanting|Enchanting XP]].

### Maximizing Enchanting Wisdom
- [Lv100] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] or higher [[Guardian Pet]] (+30 Enchanting Wisdom)
- [[Booster Cookie]] (+25 Enchanting Wisdom)
- [[God Potion]]
  - [[Potions|Enchanting XP Boost Potion III]] (+20 Enchanting Wisdom)
- [[Celestial Mason Jar]] (+3 [[Wisdom|Global Wisdom]])
- [[Quantum|Quantum V]] (+2 Enchanting Wisdom on weekends)
- [[Rift Necklace]] (+1 Enchanting Wisdom)
- [[Attributes|Book Wisdom]] attribute maxed with [[Attributes|Echo of Wisdom]] and [[Attributes|Echo of Echoes]] attributes maxed (+6.5 Enchanting Wisdom)
The maximum possible bonus Enchanting Wisdom assuming all of these effects are active would be +87.5 Enchanting Wisdom.
Excluding [[Quantum|Quantum V]], due to it's highly random nature, the highest Enchanting Wisdom which can be achieved at all times is +85.5 Enchanting Wisdom.
## Notes
- Mayor [[Derpy]] gives a 50% bonus to all Skill experience, this however does not affect the different types of [[Wisdom]].

## Notes
- The crop that gives the highest base amount of [[Farming]] XP is [[Mushroom]], at +6 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
- [[Derpy]] gives a 50% bonus to all skill XP, however this is applied independently and does not affect Wisdom Stats.
  - Events granting Skill Experience boost are multiplicative with the "MOAR SKILLZ!!!" perk, and also don't affect Farming Wisdom.

# Fear
Categories: Player Statistics
[Fear]
Type: Stat
Uses: Spawn [[Primal Fear]] more often and reduces damage dealt by them, make mobs ([[Mob]]) run away.
Increasing: Items from [[Tyashoi Alchemist]]
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: 113.3
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E004
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Cauldron ()
Fear is a [[Stats|Miscellaneous Stat]] acquired through items from [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] and used in [[The Great Spook]] event.
## Mechanics
Fear causes nearby [[Mob|Mobs]] to run away from the player if their [Lv] is less than the player's Fear when wearing [[Great Spook Armor]]. Fear also reduces the respawn time for [[Primal Fear|Primal Fears]] and reduces damage taken from Primal Fears.
Currently, the only way to increase Fear is by using items from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]].

- Fear is the only stat in the entire game that was not boosted by the [[Bal Pet]]'s old Made of Lava ability.

# Ferocity
Categories: Player Statistics, Mechanics
[Ferocity]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the chance for hits to deal damage multiple times
Increasing: [[Pets]], [[Weapons]], [[Armor]], [[Reforging]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: 500
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E00B
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Rose Red ()
Ferocity is a damage stat that increases the chance of attacks hitting multiple times.
The maximum number of hits per second with Ferocity and [[Attack Speed]] can be calculated using this calculator:
calcpage  = Calculator:Hit Count
 template  = Template:Calculator/Hit Count
 form      = hitCountCalcForm
 calcname  = Hit Count Calculator
 result    = inner
 
 param     = basehitcount|Base Hit Amount|1|int
 param     = ferocity|Ferocity|0|int
 param     = attackspeed|Attack Speed|0|int
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
## Mechanics
Each point of Ferocity grants a +1% chance for an attack to strike an extra time. For example, with 50 Ferocity it grants the player a 50% chance for their attack to hit twice. Ferocity above multiples of 100 also allow for extra triggers, meaning Ferocity above 100 allows your attack to strike three times, and so on. For example, 250 Ferocity would grant a 100% chance to strike three times, and a 50% chance to strike a fourth time.
Ferocity works on melee and arrows, but not magic.
Ferocity does not activate [[Life Steal]] or [[Drain]].
When using melee weapons, Ferocity fails to activate when the player is more than 6 blocks away from the target.

## Theoretical Maximum Ferocity
The highest possible Ferocity can be achieved using the following:
#### Temporary Boosts: 94 and +400
- [[Atomsplit Katana]] (Soulcry): +400
- [[Jerry Candy]] (included in [[God Potion]]): +2
- [[Zombie Brain Mixin]]: +10
- [[Century Cakes|Latest Update Century Cake]]: +2
- [[Hollow Wand]] (Raging Wind x2, Ichor Pool): +70
- [[Handy Blood Chalice]]: +20
#### Armor, Equipment, Held Item : 424.75 and +200
- [[Final Destination Armor]]: +200
- [[Valkyrie]]: +419
  - Base Ferocity boost, alongside five stars (+50%), five [[Master Star|Master Stars]] (+25%), five digits of secret count (+5%), and [[Dungeoneering|Dungeoneering L (50)]] (+485%): +399
  - [[Vicious|Vicious V]]: +5
  - [[Dirt Bottle]]: +15
  - [[Recombobulator 3000]]
- [[Magma Lord Necklace]]: +2
- [[Manticore Claw]]
  - Chimeric Core: +3.75
#### Accessories: +20.2
- [[Enrichments|Ferocity Enrichment]] for 66 [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] and higher accessories: 13.2 Ferocity
- [[Scorched Books|Scorching]] accessory power: +7
#### Pet
- [Lv100] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Tiger Pet]] ( [[Hephaestus Relic]]): 25×1.5 = 37.5 and +30%
This adds up to a base 576.45 , becoming 749.385 with the percentage bonus from the [[Tiger Pet]], but capped at 500 . If we include Ferocity against [[Enderman|Endermen]], which bypasses the cap but is not buffed by the [[Tiger Pet]], we end up with a total of 1100 Ferocity, meaning each hit results in 11 additional hits. Ignoring the cap entirely, we have 1176.45 Ferocity for 11 hits and a 76.45% chance to hit 12 times.
This presumes we are against a [[Fels]] in the Catacombs M7.
## Trivia
- Ferocity is disabled for Berserkers in dungeons.
- [[Speed]], [[Attack Speed]], Ferocity, [[Swing Range]], [[Mining Spread]], [[Gemstone Spread]], [[Timber]], [[Fishing Speed]], [[Trophy Chance]], [[Treasure Chance]], [[Magic Find]] and [[Charm Chance]] are the only stats with a cap.
- Ferocity against Endermen can reach numbers higher than 500.
- Killing an [[Enderman]] with a Ferocity strike causes its death sound to not play.
- Using a Bow can activate Ferocity from range.
  - This is especially noticeable in the [[Voidgloom Seraph]] fight as this ability can destroy the Hitshield rapidly. However, [[Arrows|Explosive Arrows]] or a bow that can damage Endermen is required to do this. It is recommended to use [[Juju Shortbow]] or [[Terminator]] to achieve this.
- Before Ferocity was added, the Tiger pet's ability Merciless Swipe had worked the same way like Ferocity. The former ability had a chance to trigger a second hit and was always triggered with a [Lv100] Tiger Pet.

# Fig Fortune
Categories: Player Statistics, Foraging
[Fig Fortune]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the amount of drops from Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]).
Increasing: [[Agatha]]'s Shop, [[Moonglade Talisman]] and upgrades, more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E054
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Fig Log ()
Fig Fortune is one of the player's [[Stats|gathering stats]]. It increases the chance to get multiple [[Fig Log|Fig Logs]] being chopped.
## Mechanics
100 Fig Fortune grants a chance for additional logs to be dropped. For example, a player with 150 Fig Fortune would have guaranteed double drops and a 50% chance to get triple drops.

|-|-|-|

|[[Agatha]]|Fig Fortune I-L ([[Agatha]])|+4 Fig Fortune per level, up to 200|

# Fishing Speed
Aliases: Fishing Speed Cap, Fishing speed cap
Categories: Player Statistics, Outdated pages, Article stubs, Confirmations needed, Fishing, Mechanics
[Fishing Speed]
Type: Stat
Uses: Decreases the time it takes to catch with a Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]])
Increasing: Fishing with [[Fish Affinity Talisman]], with certain [[Fishing Rods]], Fishing Baits ([[Bait]]), and [[Pets]]
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: Varies
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E00C
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Fishing Rod ()
Fishing Speed is one of the player's [[Stats|Fishing Stats]]. It decreases the amount of time it takes the player to catch an item or [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]].
## Mechanics
This article needs to be reworked as its content is outdated.
You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
Reason: New fishing speed formula
This article was last edited by Cubingisthebest (contribs|logs|block) on 5:52, October 26 2026 (31 hours ago)
The amount of ticks it will take for a fish to start approaching the bobber depends on these formulae:
BaseTicks=Random number between 200 and (400−200×LureCatchTimeReduction)Ticks=BaseTicks−(FishingSpeedFishingSpeedCap×BaseTicks)No. of Seconds=TicksTicksPerSecond FishingSpeedCap defaults to 300, but varies on some islands.
|Island|FishingSpeedCap|
|-|-|
|[[Backwater Bayou]]|200|
|[[Lotus Atoll]]|250|
|[[Crimson Isle]]|350|
|Everywhere else|300|
TicksPerSecond is 20 by default, but can vary depending on lag.
If FishingSpeed≥FishingSpeedCap, Ticks=0.
If the [[Flash]] enchantment activates, Ticks=1.
After this time is elapsed, a fish will start approaching the bobber, with a random countdown between 1 and 4 seconds before the player can reel in. This countdown is not affected by Fishing Speed, but can be reduced by up to 25% using the [[Quick Bite]] enchantment, and the [[Flash]] enchantment gives a chance to skip it, which can be increased using a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Flying Fish Pet]].

This results in a maximum of 754.68 Fishing Speed [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] on [[Jerry's Workshop]] before accounting for the bonuses from [[Bobbin' Time]]. However, this is not useful as the stat's effectiveness caps out at 300, making any excess fishing speed not useful.
## Trivia
- Fishing Speed stacks additively.
- [[Speed]], [[Attack Speed]], [[Ferocity]], [[Swing Range]], [[Mining Spread]], [[Gemstone Spread]], [[Timber]], Fishing Speed, [[Trophy Chance]], [[Treasure Chance]], [[Magic Find]] and [[Charm Chance]] are the only stats with a cap.
  - Of which [[Speed]], [[Attack Speed]] and Fishing Speed are the only ones whose cap can be changed.

# Foraging Fortune
Categories: Player Statistics, Outdated pages, Confirmations needed, Foraging
[Foraging Fortune]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the amount of drops from wood.
Increasing: [[Foraging]], [[Pets]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E054
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Player Skull ()
Foraging Fortune is one of the player's [[Stats|gathering stats]]. It increases the chance to get multiple of the log being chopped.
## Mechanics
100 Foraging Fortune grants a chance for one more log to be dropped. For example, a player with 150 Foraging Fortune would have guaranteed double drops and a 50% chance to get triple drops.
Foraging Fortune affects the following blocks:
- [[Oak Log]]
- [[Birch Log]]
- [[Spruce Log]]
- [[Dark Oak Log]]
- [[Acacia Log]]
- [[Jungle Log]]
- [[Fig Log]]
- [[Mangrove Log]]
- [[Helix Log]]

|Half Empty|7|Each player with Half Full on the island grants +1 → +25 (Max 5)|

### Starborn Temple
The Will bonus from the [[Forest Temple]] grants Foraging Fortune on the [[Galatea]] islands. Becoming a Benefactor to this temple grants you 2x that bonus. The amount of Foraging Fortune given is varied upon the amount of Benefactors that temple has.
Certain Items can be applied to [[Tools]], [[Armor]], or [[Equipment]] to increase Foraging Fortune

  - 5 [[Star Upgrades]] (+5 )
  - [[Forest Essence|Axed]] perk (+3.28 )
- [[Weather|Thunderstorm]] weather active (+25 )

##### The Park
While on [[The Park]], a [[Treecapitator]] is the highest yielding tool for Foraging Fortune
- [[Treecapitator]]

# Foraging Wisdom
Categories: Player Statistics
[Foraging Wisdom]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the Foraging XP gain.
Increasing: Foraging XP Boost Potion ([[Potions]]), [[Booster Cookie]], [[Pets]], and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Symbol: ☯
Unicode Code Point: U+262F
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Book and Quill ()
Foraging Wisdom is one of the player's [[Stats|wisdom stats]]. It increases the amount of [[Foraging|Foraging XP]] the player gains.
## Mechanics
[[Foraging|Foraging XP]] gained by the player is multiplied by 1+ForagingWisdom100. For example, if a player with 20 Foraging Wisdom gained +100 [[Foraging|Foraging XP]], they would gain 1.2x that amount, or +120 [[Foraging|Foraging XP]].
## Increasing Bonus Foraging Wisdom
### Pets
Certain [[Pets]] grant Foraging Wisdom. The [[Pets|Pet]] must be equipped in order to take effect.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ocelot Pet]]|[[Ocelot Pet]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Foraging Pet ([[Pets]])|Foraging Wisdom Boost; Grants +0 → +20 Foraging Wisdom.|
|[[Ocelot Pet]]|[[Ocelot Pet]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Foraging Pet ([[Pets]])|Foraging Wisdom Boost; Grants +0 → +25 Foraging Wisdom.|
|[[Ocelot Pet]]|[[Ocelot Pet]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Foraging Pet ([[Pets]])|Foraging Wisdom Boost; Grants +0 → +25 Foraging Wisdom.|

### Maximizing Foraging Wisdom
- [[Uncommon Foraging Wisdom Booster Upgrade]] (+15.06 Foraging Wisdom)
- [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulated]] [[Treecapitator]] or [[Helix Chopper]] with Reforge (+6 Foraging Wisdom)
  - [[Absorb|Absorb X]] (+10 Foraging Wisdom)
  - Axed perk from [[Rosemary]]'s [[Essence Shops|Forest Essence Shop]] (increases Axe stats by 2%, for a total bonus of +0.32 Foraging Wisdom)
- [Lv100] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Ocelot Pet]] (+30 Foraging Wisdom)
- [[Booster Cookie]] (+25 Foraging Wisdom)
- [[God Potion]]
  - [[Potions|Foraging XP Boost Potion III]] (+20 Foraging Wisdom)
- [[Celestial Mason Jar]] (+3 Foraging Wisdom)
- [[Abicases|Blue™ but Green Abicase]] (+1.5 Foraging Wisdom)
- [[David's Cloak]] (+5 Foraging Wisdom)
- [[Frog Mask]] (+5 Foraging Wisdom)
- [[Magic 8 Ball]] (+1 Foraging Wisdom)
- [[Quantum|Quantum V]] (+2 Foraging Wisdom)
- Efficient Forager [[Heart of the Forest]] perk (+15 Foraging Wisdom)
- Maxed [[Essence Shops|Forest Training]] while on Foraging Islands (+6 Foraging Wisdom)
- [[Attributes|Foraging Wisdom X]] attribute, boosted by [[Attributes|Echo of Wisdom]] and [[Attributes|Echo of Echoes]] (+6.5 Foraging Wisdom)
* The maximum only applies during real life weekends if [[Quantum|Quantum V]] selects Foraging Wisdom, the current SkyBlock season if [[Magic 8 Ball]] selects Foraging Wisdom and only in the selected region from the [[Frog Mask]]
## Notes
- Mayor [[Derpy]] gives a 50% bonus to all Skill experience, this however does not affect the Wisdom Stats.
  - Events granting Skill Experience boost are multiplicative with the "MOAR SKILLZ!!!" perk, and also don't affect Foraging Wisdom.

# Gemstone Fortune
Categories: Player Statistics
[Gemstone Fortune]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the amount of drops from Gemstones ([[Gemstone]])
Increasing: [[Heart of the Mountain]], Diamond Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]), [[Consumables]], [[Scatha Pet]], [[Accessories]], [[Enchantments]], [[Drills]]
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: 589
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E053
Gemstone Fortune is a Gathering [[Stats|stat]] that increase drops from [[Gemstone|Gemstones]].
## Mechanics
Every 100 Gemstone Fortune a player has increases the amount of drops received from [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] by 100%. Any excess Gemstone Fortune increases the percentage chance of receiving further drops.
For example, if a player has 130 Gemstone Fortune, they are guaranteed to receive 2x drops, with a 30% chance to receive 3x drops instead.
## Increasing Base Gemstone Fortune
Players have a base 0 Gemstone Fortune. It can be increased by the following permanent sources:
|Slot|Name|Type|Bonus|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dwarven O's Gemstone Grahams]]|[[Dwarven O's Gemstone Grahams]]|Consumable Item|2 per consumption up to 10 Gemstone Fortune|

# Gemstone Spread
Categories: Player Statistics, More info needed
[Gemstone Spread]
Type: Stat
Uses: The chance to automatically break adjacent gemstone blocks when mining.
Increasing: [[Heart of the Mountain]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: 10,000
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E00F
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Golden Pickaxe ()
Gemstone Spread is a [[Stats|Mining Stat]]. It is the chance for the player to automatically mine adjacent blocks when mining [[Gemstone|Gemstones]]. The stat is disabled on player's [[Private Island]].
## Mechanics
For values of 100 or under, Gemstone Spread represents the chance for an adjacent block to be mined. For example, a Gemstone Spread of 14 means that there is a 14% (1 in ~7.1) chance of an additional [[Gemstone]] being mined each time a Gemstone is mined manually.
At the cap of 10,000 Gemstone Spread, the player is guaranteed to automatically mine 100 extra Gemstones each time they manually mine one.
## Increasing Gemstone Spread
Gemstone Spread can be increased in various Ways:
|Slot|Name|Requirement|Gemstone Spread|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Pendant of Divan]]|[[Pendant of Divan]]|HotM X (10) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|+2.5 in [[Glacite Mineshafts]]|
|Name|Requirement|Gemstone Spread|
|-|-|-|
|Steady Hand ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|HotM VIII (8) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|+0.1-10.1 in [[Glacite Mineshafts]]|
|Slot|Name|Source|Gemstone Spread|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Will-o'-wisp]]|[[Will-o'-wisp]]|Forging at HotM X (10) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|+2.5|
|Slot|Name|Gemstone Spread|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Littlefoot Shard]]|[[Littlefoot Shard]]|+0.5 per +29-20 [[Cold Resistance]] while in [[Glacite Mineshafts]]|Catch [[Littlefoot]] with Pocket Black Hole ([[Black Holes]]) Kill [[Littlefoot]] while using [[Salts]]|
|[[Gemzie Shard]]|[[Gemzie Shard]]|Grants +0.25-2.5 Gemstone Spread|Catch a [[Gemzie]] with [[Critter Capsule]] in the Cavern biome of the [[Critter Safari]].|
## Maximising Gemstone Spread
- Wearing a [[Pendant of Divan]] inside a [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. (2.5 )
- Steady Hand [[Heart of the Mountain]] perk at level 50 while holding a drill with [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]] applied and being inside a [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. (10.1 )
- Being under the effect of [[Will-o'-wisp]]. (2.5 )
- [[Attributes|Freezing Spread]] attribute at level 10, while having 180 [[Cold Resistance]] or more. (4.5 )
  - While [[Candidate Jerry]] is mayor, players can get more than 200 [[Cold Resistance]] (5 )-->
- [[Attributes|Devouring Spread]] attribute at level 10. (2.5 )
With all of the above, the player can have up to 22.1 Gemstone Spread or 22.6 Gemstone Spread when [[Candidate Jerry]] is elected, granting 22.1% (1 in ~4.5) or 22.6% (1 in ~4.4) to break an additional [[Gemstone]] when mining.
## Trivia
- After the release of the 0.20.6 update, every player who had active [[Century Cakes|Century Cake]] buff, would also have 5 Gemstone Spread, 5 [[Mining Spread]] and 5 [[Pristine]] due to a bug, it was later fixed. [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When was this fixed?; When this info is added, remove this.)

  - Gemstone Spread and [[Mining Spread]] have the highest cap of all stats — 10,000.

# Health
Aliases: HP, Health Cost, Maximum Health
Categories: Player Statistics, Article stubs, Confirmations needed, Mechanics, Health
[Health]
Type: Stat
Uses: Determining player's max HP
Increasing: SkyBlock Level ([[SkyBlock Levels]]), [[Armor]], [[Enchantments]], Reforges ([[Reforging]]), [[Pets]], [[Farming]], [[Fishing]], Hot Potato Books ([[Hot Potato Book]]) and more
[Values]
Base Value: 100
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E010
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Golden Apple ()
Health (HP) is one of the eleven core player [[Stats|statistics]]. It determines players' and mobs' max health.
Health is also referred to as "Max Health" or "Max HP".
Alongside [[Intelligence]], it is the only stat that is increased by multiple skills (Farming, Fishing and Carpentry).
## Mechanics
Health represents the health points a player has. When Health reaches 0, the player dies. The amount of Health a player has is displayed as hearts. By default, 10 hearts are shown. This amount increases up to 20 (2 rows of 10) as Health increases.
The player regenerates Health at an interval. The amount of Health the player regenerates is determined by their [[Health Regen]].
## Increasing Base Health
Players have a base Health of 100 by default. It can be increased permanently by:
|Source|HP Gain|Total|
|-|-|-|
|[[Farming]] level Level 1–14|+2 per level|+242 at level 60|
|[[Farming]] level Level 15–19|+3 per level|+242 at level 60|
|[[Farming]] level Level 20–25|+4 per level|+242 at level 60|
|[[Farming]] level Level 26–60|+5 per level|+242 at level 60|
|[[Fishing]] level Level 1–14|+2 per level|+192 at level 50|
|[[Fishing]] level Level 15–19|+3 per level|+192 at level 50|
|[[Fishing]] level Level 20–25|+4 per level|+192 at level 50|
|[[Fishing]] level Level 26–50|+5 per level|+192 at level 50|
|[[Carpentry]] level Each level|+1 per level|+50 at level 50|
|[[Zombie Slayer]] level Each level|+2–6|+34 at level 9|
|[[Wolf Slayer]] level Levels 2, 4, 6, 9|+2–5|+12 at level 9|
|[[Enderman Slayer]] level Levels 1, 3, 5, 7, 9|+1–5|+15 at level 9|
|[[Blaze Slayer]] level Levels 1, 3, 5, 7, 9|+3–7|+25 at level 9|
|[[Bestiary]] milestone level Each level|+2 per level|+736 at max level 368|
|[[Catacombs]] level Each Level|+2 per level|+100 at level 50|
|Forbidden Health ([[Essence Shops]]) perk Each level|+2 per level|+10 at level 5|
|Blessing of Time ([[Oruo The Almighty]]) perk Each level|+5 per level|+15 at level 3|
|[[SkyBlock Levels]] All levels|+5 per level|+3410 at level 620|
|[[SkyBlock Levels]] Every 10 levels|+10 per level|+3410 at level 620|
|[[9f™ Network]] Each level|+2–18|+18 at relay 9|
|Consuming Reaper Peppers ([[Reaper Pepper]]) Eating|+1|+5 at 5 eaten|
|[[Carrolyn]] Exporting items|+5 per set of items|+40 after 8 items exported|

### Absorption Health
Absorption health is temporary and does not regenerate once lost. It can be gained using [[Potions]] or some abilities such as the one of [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Wither Shield]] scroll.
## Healing
Healing regenerates Health over time up to its maximum value.
## Theoretical Maximum
- Base Health ( 100 Health )
- Maxed out [[Farming]], [[Fishing]], and [[Carpentry]] skills ( 242 Health + 192 Health + 50 Health = 484 Health )
- [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer IX (9)]], [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer IX (9)]], [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer IX (9)]], [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer IX (9)]] ( 34 Health + 12 Health + 15 Health + 25 Health = 86 Health )
- Maxed [[Bestiary]] ( 736 Health )
- [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 568]] ( 2,840 Health )
- [[9f™ Network]], [[Essence Shops|Wither Essence Shop]], [[Oruo The Almighty|Blessing of Time]], [[Reaper Pepper]] ( 18 Health + 10 Health + 15 Health + 5 Health = 48 Health )
- Hypermaxed [[Fervor Armor]] ( 4,730.2 Health )
- [[High Class Archfiend Dice]] ( 300 Health )
- [[God Potion]] with [[Deepterror Mixin]], [[Beacon|Beacon V]] with [[Scorched Power Crystal]], [[Century Cakes|Crab-Colored Century Cake]] ( 100 Health + 210 Health + 60 Health + 10 Health = 380 Health )
- 10✪ [[Lava Shell Necklace]], [[Dragonfade Cloak]], [[Ender Belt]] and [[Ender Gauntlet]], Fully enchanted with [[Cayenne]] and [[Prosperity]], reforged to [[Blaze Wax|Waxed]]. ( 499 Health )
- [[Accessory Power|Healthy Accessory Power]] with [[Accessory Power|2,050 Accessory Power]], all Tunings and enrichments set to HP and all [[Accessories]]. ( 1,954 Health + 1,025 Health + 195 Health + [[Accessories]] Health = 2,481 Health ) [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- Holding [[Reforging|Bulky]] [[Daedalus Blade]] enchanted with [[Chimera|Chimera V]] while having [Lv100] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Blue Whale Pet]] with [[Minos Relic]] equipped. ( 800 Health )
- [[Candidate Jerry]] ( +10% Health )
- Archimedes perk of the [Lv100] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Blue Whale Pet]] ( +20% Health )
The combination of this results in 17,772.56 HP while in [[The End]].
This total is a lower bound. It does not include the Health gained from [[Attributes|Nature Elemental]] and the other Elemental attributes, the [[Hephaestus Relic]] pet item bonus, [[David's Cloak]] or the [[Rift Necklace]]'s growth per Timecharm.
However, you can reach much higher health using [[Mastiff Armor]] and a high [[Crit Damage]] weapon while in a dungeon.
## Trivia
- The Health given by the [[Dungeoneering]] skill is only given while in dungeons.
- Amount of Hearts on the action bar depends on player's Health:

|-|-|
|Less than 125 Health|10|
|Less than 165 Health|11|
|Less than 230 Health|12|
|Less than 300 Health|13|
|Less than 400 Health|14|
|Less than 500 Health|15|
|Less than 650 Health|16|
|Less than 800 Health|17|
|Less than 1,000 Health|18|
|Less than 1,250 Health|19|
|More than 1,250 Health|20|

# Hearts
Categories: Player Statistics, Mechanics, Rift Dimension
[Hearts]
Type: Stat
Uses: Determines the amount of Health players have in the Rift.
Increasing: [[Armor]], [[Rift Necklace]], [[Vampire Dentist Relic]]
[Values]
Base Value: 10
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E01F
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Apple ()
Hearts is a [[Stats|Rift Stat]]. It determines how much damage the player can take before dying.
## Mechanics
The player has a base 10 Hearts, which can be increased through various items. The more Hearts the player has, the more damage they can take in the [[Stillgore Château]] or [[Time Chamber]] before being sent to the [[Oubliette]] or failing the fight.
Hearts are present throughout the entirety of the [[Rift Dimension]], but will only take effect in the [[Stillgore Château]] or [[Time Chamber]].
## Increasing Bonus Hearts
### Armor
Some sets of [[Armor]] grant Hearts while equipped.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Anti-Bite Scarf]]|[[Anti-Bite Scarf]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])/MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Helmet ([[Helmets]])|+1|
|[[Wizard Face]]|[[Wizard Face]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Helmet ([[Helmets]])|+1|
|[[Warm Wizard Face]]|[[Warm Wizard Face]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Helmet ([[Helmets]])|+1|
|[[Warmer Wizard Face]]|[[Warmer Wizard Face]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Helmet ([[Helmets]])|+1|
|[[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]]|[[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Chestplate ([[Chestplates]])|+1|
|[[W Logo]]|[[W Logo]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Chestplate ([[Chestplates]])|+1|
|[[Leggings of the Coven]]|[[Leggings of the Coven]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|[[Leggings]]|+1|
|[[Wizardman Leggings]]|[[Wizardman Leggings]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Leggings]]|+1|
### Equipment
Some pieces of [[Equipment]] grant Hearts while equipped.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||Requirements|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Rift Necklace]]|[[Rift Necklace]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Necklace ([[Necklaces]])|+1|Secured at least 7/8 Timecharms ([[Rift Timecharms]])|
### Accessories
Certain [[Accessories]] grant Hearts. They must be in the [[Player]]'s inventory while in the [[Rift Dimension]] to take effect.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type||
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Vampire Dentist Relic]]|[[Vampire Dentist Relic]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Accessory ([[Accessories]])|+1|
### Maximizing Hearts
- Base Hearts (+10 )
- [[Anti-Bite Scarf]], [[Wizard Face]], [[Warm Wizard Face]] or [[Warmer Wizard Face]] (+1 )
- [[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]] or [[W Logo]] (+1 )
- [[Leggings of the Coven]] or [[Wizardman Leggings]] (+1 )
- [[Rift Necklace]] (+1 )
- [[Vampire Dentist Relic]] (+1 )
This totals to 15 Hearts.

|[[Anti-Bite Scarf]]|[[Anti-Bite Scarf]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Helmet|-0.5|
|[[Warm Wizard Face]]|[[Warm Wizard Face]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Helmet|-0.3|
|[[Warmer Wizard Face]]|[[Warmer Wizard Face]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Helmet|-0.5|
## Decreasing Bonus Hearts
### Mobs
Certain mobs' attack decreases bonus hearts. These reduce the Hearts cap and thus cannot be regenerated from using any Healing Melon or reduced. Instead, various methods have to be used to regenerate those Hearts.
|Mob|Description|Method to regenerate decreased Hearts|
|-|-|-|
|Clotgoyle ([[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]])|-1 Hearts per Clotgoyle alive per hit|Hearts are given back to players when killed|
|[[Splatter]]|-8 Hearts on reaching 30% health|Hearts are given back to players by collecting the hearts particles splattered nearby|
### Hearts Damage
Certain mobs' attack decreases hearts. These can be regenerated from using any Healing Melon or reduced.
|Mob|Attack|Damage per hit|
|-|-|-|
|[[Thrall]]|Normal Attack|-0.5 Hearts|
|[[Fledgling]]|Normal Attack|-1 Hearts|
|[[Splatter]]|Melee Attack|-1 Hearts|
|[[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]]|Coagulation|-0.5 Hearts for Tier I, -1 Hearts for Tier II, Tier III, Tier IV and -1.5 Hearts for Tier V|
|[[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]]|Mania(in attack range but not in correct ring)|-2 Hearts for Tier I, -3 Hearts for Tier II, -5 Hearts for Tier III, -6 Hearts forTier IV and -15 Hearts for Tier V|
|[[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]]|Mania(out of attack range)|-3 Hearts for Tier I, -3 Hearts for Tier II, -3 Hearts for Tier III, -6 Hearts forTier IV and -6 Hearts for Tier V|
|[[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]]|Stonewraith|-5 Hearts|
|[[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]]|Twinclaws|-3 Hearts for Tier II, -5 Hearts for Tier III, -6 Hearts forTier IV and -8 Hearts for Tier V|
|[[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]]|Killer Spring|-100 Hearts if not destroyed in 15 seconds|
|[[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]]|Impel|-4 Hearts for Tier III, -5 Hearts forTier IV and -20 Hearts for Tier V|
|[[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]]|Blood Ichor|-0.5 Hearts per second per existing Blood Ichor, -100 Hearts if not destroyed in 25 seconds|

# Heat
Categories: Player Statistics, More info needed
[Heat]
Type: Stat
Uses: Starts dealing [[True Damage]] at 100; Boosting [[Mining Speed]] and [[Mining Fortune]]; Boosting [[Stoneworm]] spawns
Increasing: Being in the [[Magma Fields]] or [[Khazad-dûm]]
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: 100
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E012
Heat is a mechanic in the [[Magma Fields]] and [[Khazad-dûm]] that damages the player upon reaching 100 .
## Mechanics
Heat evolves between 0 and +100.
### Effects of Heat
- At +90 , the player is set on [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Fire]].
- At +100 , the player start taking increasing chunks of [[True Damage]].
  - This ignores [[True Defense]].[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; How much ? What eprcantage ? Can it be decreased with heat resistance ?; When this info is added, remove this.)
  - This is negated from [[Ember Armor]]'s Full Set Bonus: Nether Lord.
- Every +1 increases the chance of spawning a [[Stoneworm]] (and inherently, a [[Scatha]]) by +0.05%, per piece of [[Equipment]] reforged to [[Reforging|Blazing]] ( [[Blazen Sphere]]).
- Every +1 grants +4 [[Mining Speed]] and +1 [[Mining Fortune]], using a [[Tools|Mining Tool]] reforged to [[Reforging|Heated]] ( [[Scorched Topaz]]).
### Increasing Heat
Heat increases while the player is (A) at [[Khazad-dûm]] or (B) at [[Coordinates|X, 64, Z]] or (C) in the [[Magma Fields]] at [[Coordinates|X, 63, Z]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
- Heat accumulates at a rate of +x every "Heat Tick", happening every y seconds:
  - y=1×(1+Heat Resistance)
  +x Heat: +0.1, Source: X, 64, Z ([[Coordinates]]) in the [[Crystal Hollows]] or X, 63, Z ([[Coordinates]]) and below in the [[Magma Fields]]; +x Heat: +0.2, Source: [[Khazad-dûm]] (overrides the above); +x Heat: +1, Source: Being on Fire ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]); +x Heat: +3, Source: Lava ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]) at X, 64, Z ([[Coordinates]]) in the [[Crystal Hollows]] or X, 63, Z ([[Coordinates]]) and below in the [[Magma Fields]] (overrides the above); +x Heat: Heat Resistance, Source: Source; +x Heat: +0.01, Source: For every 1 [[Heat Resistance]].; +x Heat: ∞, Source: [[Heat Armor]]'s Full Set Bonus: Heat Shield or [[Ember Armor]]'s Full Set Bonus: Nether Lord unless set on Fire ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]) from Lava ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]) for the latter.
- Heat can be entirely negated by wearing the full [[Heat Armor]].
### Decreasing Heat
Heat decreases everywhere else or while wearing the full [[Heat Armor]].
- Heat depletes at a rate of -1 every 1 second, unaffected by [[Heat Resistance]].
- Heat can also be decreased by a flat amount from the following:
  Source: [[Tungsten Regulator]], Type of source: Drill Part ([[Drill Parts]]), [[Cold]]: -1 / 100 Fuel ([[Machine Fuel]]) consumed on the Drill ([[Drills]]).; Source: EPIC ([[Rarity]])+ [[Bal Pet]]'s Dispersion, Type of source: Pet ([[Pets]]) perk, [[Cold]]: -0.04 → -4 / [[Mob]] kill, Condition: [[Crystal Hollows]]; Source: Water ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]), Type of source: Location, [[Cold]]: -5 / second.; Source: Changing Worlds, Type of source: Location, [[Cold]]: Set to 0; Source: Dying, [[Cold]]: Set to 0; Source: Drinking a [[Water Bottle]] or a Potion ([[Potions]]), [[Cold]]: -10
## Game Messages
|Condition|Game Message|
|-|-|
|Entering the [[Magma Fields]]|DANGER; You have entered the Magma Fields|
|Reaching +90 Heat|The following titles sequentially appear, spaced by a few seconds: You are heating up! You are starting to burn! You are melting! Time to leave!|
|Dying from reaching +100 Heat|You burned to death.|
## Trivia
- It used to be possible to reach 104 Heat by being in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]].

## Increasing Critter Safari Hunting Fortune
Most sources of Hunting Fortune do not apply within the [[Critter Safari]]. Only this list applies to it:
- [[Safari Belt]] – up to +30 with [[Andrea|Haunted Biome Milestone X (10)]]
  - [[Reforging|Reforged]] with [[Reforging|Majestic]] – up to +4
- [[Safari Essence|Hunting Hotspot]] – up to +25 while within the highlighted zone
- [[Eagle Pet]] - up to +10 with 4 Eagle pet owners in Critter Safari
Maximum Hunting Fortune in [[Critter Safari]] is 69 in highlighted zone
## Maximum
The maximum achievable Hunting Fortune without any conditional buffs and without Benefactor Bonuses from the [[Starborn Temples]] is 181 .
- [[Hunting|Hunting L (50)]] – +50
- Hunter's Luck perk in [[Heart of the Forest]] – +50
- [[David's Cloak]] – +30 with [[David Hunterborough|Attribute Stacks Milestone XXX (30)]]
- [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Safari Belt]] – +30 with [[Andrea|Haunted and Cavern Biome Milestones X (10)]]
  - [[Reforging|Reforged]] with [[Reforging|Majestic]] – +4
- [[Infernal Kuudra Core]] – +3
- [[Attributes|Hunter's Karma X]] – +13 – including boosts from [[Attributes|Echo of Hunter X]] and [[Attributes|Echo of Echoes X]]
- [[Century Cakes|Starborn Century Cake]] – +1
The maximum conditional Hunting Fortune without Benefactor Bonuses from the [[Starborn Temples]] is 244.08 when hunting [[Critters]] that drop [[Rarity|COMMON]] or [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Combat Shards]].
- Maxed [[Bestiary]] for any [[Critters|Critter]] – +10
- [[Large Pocket Black Hole]] – +5 (On [[Attributes|Combat Shards]])
- Maxed [[Attributes|Decent Karma]] or [[Attributes|Good Karma]] – +20 – not affected by [[Attributes|Echo of Hunter]]
- Maxed [[Attributes|Hunter's Grasp]] – +13% (+28.08 ) – including boosts from [[Attributes|Echo of Hunter X]] and [[Attributes|Echo of Echoes X]]
Currently, it is impossible to meet all of these conditions, as there is no [[Critters|Critter]] outside of the [[Critter Safari]] that drops a [[Attributes|Combat Shard]]. This means that the practical maximum conditional Hunting Fortune is:
- 232.78 when using a [[Large Pocket Black Hole]] on a mob that drops [[Rarity|COMMON]] or [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Combat Shards]].
- 223.5 when using a [[Extremely Mild Adhesive|Sticky]] [[Gigantic Fishing Net]] on a [[Critters|Critter]] that drops [[Rarity|COMMON]] or [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Water Shards]].
## Trivia
- On the initial alpha release, the stat was called Syphon Luck and granted a chance to double shards when syphoning rather than obtaining.
  - This was changed due to concerns such as many players deliberately holding off on syphoning shards acquired from old equipment until they reached a higher amount of Syphon Luck.

# Hunting Wisdom

[Hunting Wisdom]
