Hunting Wisdom is one of the player's [[Stats|wisdom stats]]. It increases the amount of [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] the player gains.
## Mechanics
[[Hunting|Hunting XP]] gained by the player is multiplied by 1+HuntingWisdom100. For example, if a player with 20 Hunting Wisdom gained +100 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]], they would gain 1.2x that amount, or +120 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]].
## Increasing Bonus Hunting Wisdom
- [[Booster Cookie]] - +25
- [[Celestial Mason Jar]] - +3
- [[Attributes|Hunt Wisdom]] - +0.5 per level - up to +5 at Level X (10)
- [[Attributes|Echo of Wisdom]] - buff [[Attributes|Hunt Wisdom]] by 2% per level - up to +1 at Level X (10)
- [[Attributes|Echo of Echoes]] - buff [[Attributes|Echo of Wisdom]] by 5% per level - up to +0.5 at Level X (10)
- [[David's Cloak]] - up to +5 with [[David Hunterborough|Attribute Menu Milestone XXVI (26)]]
- [[Safari Belt]] - up to +5 Hunting Wisdom at Cavern Biome Milestone IX (9)
- [[Morning Dew|Majestic]] reforge - up to +4 Hunting Wisdom at [[Rarity|Mythic]] rarity
- [[Boosters]] - +0.5/+1/+2 for a [[Common Hunting Wisdom Booster]], or +1/+2/+3 for an [[Uncommon Hunting Wisdom Booster Upgrade]]
### Maximizing Hunting Wisdom

- [[Celestial Mason Jar]] - +3

- [[Attributes|Echo of Wisdom]] X (10) - +1
- [[Attributes|Echo of Echoes]] X (10) - +0.5
- [[David's Cloak]] - +6
  - Base +5 Hunting Wisdom at [[David Hunterborough|Attribute Menu Milestone XXVI (26)]]
  - +1 Hunting Wisdom from an [[Uncommon Hunting Wisdom Booster Upgrade]]

- +8 Hunting Wisdom from a full set of [[Foraging]] armor equipped with [[Uncommon Hunting Wisdom Booster Upgrade|Uncommon Hunting Wisdom Booster Upgrades]]
- +3.06 Hunting Wisdom from an [[Axes|Axe]] with an [[Uncommon Hunting Wisdom Booster Upgrade]] with the [[Rosemary|Axed Essence Shop Perk]].
This results in a total of 63.56 Hunting Wisdom when holding an [[Axes|Axe]] with hunting [[Boosters]]

# Knockback
Aliases: Knockback Resistance, Knockback resistance, True Knockback
Categories: Player Statistics, Article stubs, Mechanics
[Knockback]
Knockback Stat Icon.png This stat has not seen an in-game icon. This icon is chosen for wiki use.
Type: Stat
Knockback is a game mechanic that pushes a player or mob away when they are hit.
## Dealing Knockback
- [[Knockback (Enchantment)|Knockback]] enchantment (applied to [[Swords]])
- [[Punch]] enchantment (applied to [[Bows]])
- [[Knockback Potion]]
- [[Aspect of the Dragons]] - Dragon Rage ability applies large Knockback to all mobs in a radius.
## Knockback Resistance
- [[Slime Hat]] - provides immunity to Knockback from mobs.
- [[Dwarf Turtle Shelmet]] and [[Hephaestus Shelmet]] - a [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that provides immunity to Knockback alongside some [[True Defense]].
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Turtle Pet]] - Unflippable ability provides immunity to Knockback.
- [[Rotten Armor]] - Each piece of Rotten Armor grants 15% knockback resistance to arrows, with its Sieve Body full set bonus granting an additional +20% knockback resistance to arrows.
- [[Tank]] - Class Passive: Defensive Stance Grants immunity to Knockback from mobs.
- [[Attributes|Shell]]: 10-100% chance to negate Knockback.
  - At level 10 this grants immunity to all Knockback.
## True Knockback
[[Knockback|True Knockback]] is a type of Knockback that ignores knockback resistance. Certain sources of True Knockback are intentional, while others may be bugs.
### Sources
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
- [[Bonzo]]'s explosive balloons (intentional)
- [[Dropship]] TNT (intentional)
- [[Power Dragon]]'s TNT Bomb (intentional)
- [[Kuudra Knocker]] (likely intentional)
- [[Bigfoot]]'s Stomp attack
- [[Carrot King]]
- [[Cretan Bull]]
- [[Ender Dragon]] spawning
- Fireballs and Wither Skulls
  - e.g.: [[Inferno Magma Cube]], [[Lava Pigman]], [[Magma Boss]]
- [[Guardian (disambiguation)|Guardian]] attacks
  - e.g.: [[The Professor]], [[Thunder]]
- [[Lost Adventurer]]'s Dragon's Breath ability
- [[Nightmare]]
- [[Phantom Fisher]]
## Tips
- The [[Knockback (Enchantment)|Knockback]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] is generally undesired as pushing enemies away often increases the time to kill them. In most cases, the default knockback applied by weapons is considered sufficient.
  - Players may want to put [[Knockback (Enchantment)|Knockback II]] on a secondary weapon, such as an [[Aspect of the End]], for cases where they want to knock away mobs.
## Trivia
- The swords in the [[Dojo]]'s Test of Control come pre-enchanted with [[Knockback (Enchantment)|Knockback III]], a normally unobtainable level.
## History

|Jan 5, 2023|Fixed knockback immunity overriding other knockback reduction/immunity effects.|

# Mana
Aliases: Mana Pool, Mana pool, Maximum Mana, Overflow, Overflow Mana
Categories: Player Statistics, Mana, Intelligence, Mechanics
[Mana]
Type: Stat
Uses: Item Abilities
Increasing: Increasing Int ([[Intelligence]]); [[Pets]]
[Values]
Base Value: 100
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E003
Type: Stat
Uses: As Mana when Mana Pool exhausted; Item Abilities
Increasing: [[Soulflow Pile]] accessory line
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: Method dependent; Currently 780, through [[Soulflow Supercell]] or [[Gloomlock Grimoire]], with the [[End Portal Fumes]] [[God Potion]] effect
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E017
Mana is a player resource with its maximum value increased with [[Intelligence]]. It is used to activate various item abilities.
[[Mana|Overflow Mana]] is a separate player resource that can be used like Mana when Mana is exhausted.
## Mechanics
The Current Mana, Maximum Mana, and [[Mana|Overflow Mana]] stats can be viewed on the main information bar, along with [[Defense]] and [[Health]].
### Mana
Most Mana abilities have a fixed Mana Cost, such as the [[Aspect of the Dragons]], while some such as the [[End Stone Sword]] use a percentage of the Maximum Mana. This Mana Cost is deducted from the Mana pool upon usage. If there is less Mana in the pool than the ability requires, the ability will not work.
Upon consumption of Mana, the player's Mana Pool will refill every second according to the Mana Regeneration Rate until it reaches the maximum.
#### Mana Pool
The Mana Pool holds all the Mana that the player has. It can store up to a finite amount of Mana. The Current Mana or Mana is the existing Mana in a player's Mana Pool. The Maximum Mana, or the size of Mana Pool, is the maximum amount of Mana that the Mana Pool can store.
The base value of Maximum Mana is 100. Each point of [[Intelligence]] increases the Maximum Mana by 1.
#### Mana Regeneration
Mana regenerates every second until it reaches the maximum.
The base value is 2% of the Maximum Mana per second.
When the Maximum Mana is decreased past the Current Mana, due to losing [[Intelligence]] or otherwise, the extra Mana above the new Maximum Mana is removed.
### Overflow Mana
[[Mana|Overflow Mana]] is a bonus resource that is used as Mana only when the Mana Pool is exhausted. When attempting to use abilities with insufficient Mana in the Mana Pool, it will take [[Mana|Overflow Mana]].
[[Mana|Overflow Mana]] does not naturally regenerate. It must be directly converted from internalized [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] with the [[Soulflow Pile]] Accessory line or other means (See [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow (Mechanic)#Obtaining]]). Unlike Mana, [[Mana|Overflow Mana]] does not reset when switching lobbies.
Currently, all methods of conversion sets the player's [[Mana|Overflow Mana]] to a certain value, overwriting any existing [[Mana|Overflow Mana]].
## Ways to Increase Mana Regeneration
Ways to increase [[Mana|Maximum Mana]]:
- Every [[Intelligence|Int]] increases maximum Mana by 1.
Ways to increase Mana Regeneration:
[[Pets]]:
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Guardian Pet]]: +0-30% (0.3% per pet level), doubled in or near [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]].
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Wisp Pet]]: +0-40% (0.4% per pet level).
[[Potions]]:
- [[Mana Potion]]: +1-8 Mana per second (1 per potion level).
[[Deployables]]:
- [[Mana Flux Power Orb]]: 1.5x
- [[Overflux Power Orb]]: 2x
- [[Plasmaflux Power Orb]]: 2.25x
- [[Alert Flare]]: 1.5x
- [[SOS Flare]]: 2.25x
[[Attributes]]:
- [[Attributes|Mana Regeneration]]: +1-10% (1% per level).
Ways to increase the value of [[Mana|Overflow Mana]]:
[[Accessories]]:
- [[Soulflow Pile]]: Convert 5 [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] into 200 [[Mana|Overflow Mana]].
- [[Soulflow Battery]]: Convert 4 [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] into 400 [[Mana|Overflow Mana]].
- [[Soulflow Supercell]]: Convert 4 [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] into 600 [[Mana|Overflow Mana]].
Other Items:
- [[Gloomlock Grimoire]]: Convert 15 [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] to gain up to 600 Overflow, but lose 800 [[Health]] .
[[Mixins]]:
- [[End Portal Fumes]]: When added to [[God Potion]] and consumed, all [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] conversions provide +30% [[Mana|Overflow Mana]].
## Max "Maximum Mana"
[[Mana|Maximum Mana]] is equals to Maximum [[Intelligence]] + 100 Mana.
## Max Overflow Mana
The maximum [[Mana|Overflow Mana]] can be obtained through a conversion method with the highest [[Mana|Overflow Mana]] value.
The current maximum is 780, obtained through [[Soulflow Supercell]] or [[Gloomlock Grimoire]] (600), with the [[End Portal Fumes]] [[God Potion]] effect (+30%).
## Trivia
- Due to a bug, it is possible to have a negative amount of [[Mana|Overflow Mana]]. This effectively subtracts the [[Mana|Overflow Mana]] value from the Mana pool, rendering any abilities which cost more than the difference unable to be used. This can be fixed by gaining [[Mana|Overflow Mana]] with a [[Soulflow Pile]] or other means.
  - If the negative [[Mana|Overflow Mana]] exceeds Mana, no abilities will be able to be used, including abilities which do not cost Mana.

# Mining Spread
Aliases: Block Spread, Block spread, Spread
Categories: Player Statistics
[Mining Spread]
Type: Stat
Uses: The chance to automatically break adjacent blocks when mining.
Increasing: [[Heart of the Mountain]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: 10000
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E016
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Golden Pickaxe ()
Mining Spread is a [[Stats|Mining Stat]]. It is the chance for the player to automatically mine adjacent blocks when mining Blocks, [[Ores]], and Dwarven Metals.
## Mechanics
For values of 100 or under, Mining Spread represents the chance for an adjacent block to be mined. For example, a Mining Spread of 14 means that there is a 14% chance of an additional block being mined each time a block.
For values over 100, the first digit (for a 3-digit value), or first two digits (for a 4-digit value) represent the number of adjacent blocks that are guaranteed to be broken. The following two digits represent the chance of an extra adjacent block being mined. For example, a Mining Spread of 145 means that it is guaranteed for 1 adjacent block to be mined, with a 45% chance of an extra being mined as well, whilst a Mining Spread of 1231 would guarantee 12 blocks to be mined, with a 31% chance of one extra one.
At the cap of 10,000 Mining Spread, the player is guaranteed to automatically mine 100 extra blocks each time they mine a block.
Additionally, Mining Spread will not break blocks beneath the player's feet, even if there are no other options. This is ignored if the player broke a block with positive (downwards) pitch. While this helps in most cases (namely with [[Hard Stone]]), it can be confusing in other cases, with mining spread "not working" at times. Blocks mined from Mining Spread will also grant [[Skills|Skill XP]].

## Achieving Maximum Mining Spread
By getting all of the following, it is possible to obtain a maximum of 1263 Mining Spread while mining blocks.
- Using a recombobulated [[Diamonite|Fleet]] (+150) [[Divan's Drill]] with a [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]] (+3).
- Using a full set of [[Glossy Mineral Armor]] (+600).
- Leveling the Efficient Miner perk in the [[Heart of the Mountain]] to level 100 (+300).
- Sleepy Spread X (+10).
- Using the ability Sheer Force (+200).
When mining [[Hard Stone]], an extra 401.76 Mining Spread can be achieved by leveling the Mole perk in the [[Heart of the Mountain]] to level 200. Additionally, the [[Snail Pet|LEGENDARY Snail Pet's]] perk "Slimy Reach" gives 400 more bringing the maximum Mining Spread for [[Hard Stone]] to 2064.76 Mining Spread.
## Trivia
- [[Speed]], [[Attack Speed]], [[Ferocity]], [[Swing Range]], Mining Spread, [[Gemstone Spread]], [[Timber]], [[Fishing Speed]], [[Trophy Chance]], [[Treasure Chance]], [[Magic Find]] and [[Charm Chance]] are the only stats with a cap.
  - Mining Spread and [[Gemstone Spread]] have the highest cap of all stats — 10,000.

# Mining Wisdom
Categories: Player Statistics
[Mining Wisdom]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the Mining XP gain.
Increasing: Mining XP Boost Potion ([[Potions]]), [[Booster Cookie]], [[Pets]], [[Heart of the Mountain]], [[Compact]], [[Reforging]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Symbol: ☯
Unicode Code Point: U+262F
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Book and Quill ()
Mining Wisdom is one of the player's [[Stats|wisdom stats]]. It increases the amount of [[Mining|Mining XP]] the player gains.
## Mechanics
[[Mining|Mining XP]] gained by the player is multiplied by 1+MiningWisdom100. For example, if a player with 20 Mining Wisdom gained +100 [[Mining|Mining XP]], they would gain 1.2x that amount, or +120 [[Mining|Mining XP]].

# Ore Fortune
Categories: Player Statistics
[Ore Fortune]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the amount of drops from [[Ores]]
Increasing: [[Heart of the Mountain]], [[Consumables]], [[Goblin Pet]]
[Values]
Base Value: 0
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E053
Ore Fortune is a [[Stats|gathering stat]] that increase drops from [[Ores]]. It has no effect while on [[Private Island]].
## Mechanics
Every 100 Ore Fortune a player has increases the amount of drops received from [[Ores]] by 100%. Any excess Ore Fortune increases the percentage chance of receiving further drops.
For example, if a player has 130 Ore Fortune, they are guaranteed to receive 2x drops, with a 30% chance to receive 3x drops instead.
## Increasing Base Ore Fortune
Players have a base 0 Ore Fortune. It can be increased by the following permanent sources:

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dwarven O's Ore Oats]]|[[Dwarven O's Ore Oats]]|Consumable Item|2 per consumption up to 10 Ore Fortune|

# Overbloom
Aliases: Rare Crop Chance
Categories: Player Statistics
[Overbloom]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases chances of dropping RARE CROPS.
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: Uncapped
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E02B
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Cactus Flower ()
Overbloom is a stat that increases the chances of dropping [[Rare Crops]] from farming or vacuuming [[Pests]]. It has no effect while on the [[Private Island]].
## Mechanics
The formula for the drop chance of [[Rare Crops]] including Overbloom is:
Chance=BaseChance×(1+Overbloom100)

## Theoretical Maximum Overbloom
### Universal Sources
#### Armor and Equipment
- Utilizing any armor set, enchanted with [[Sunset|Sunset V]] during the day (+20 )
  - This armor set needs to be enchanted with [[Thorns|Thorns IV]]
  - The player will need to use a [[Pufferfish Hat]] that came from a [[Raffle of the Century|Raffle]], considering it comes already with [[Thorns|Thorns V]]
- Using a set of Equipment that is reforged to [[Reforging|Thorny]] and has a base [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] (to be recombobulated to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]])
  - With the armor set described above, a player should have 17 tiers of [[Thorns]] on the armor (+12.8 )
#### Miscellaneous
- [[Rose Dragon Pet|Lv200 Rose Dragon Pet]] (+40 )
  - [[Poignant Lucky Clover]] (+13 )
- [[Freshly Baked Heirloom]] (+5 )
- [[Garden Chips|Lv20 Rarefinder Chip]] (+50 )
- [[Attributes|Crop Bug X]] ( [[Beetle Shard]]) (+10 )
- Consuming five [[Feast Burger with a Side of Deepfries]] (+5 )
This brings the total Non-Temp to 155.8 Overbloom
### Temporary Sources
- [[Zorro's Cape]] perk during a Jacob's Contest (+3.2 )
- [[Freshly Baked Heirloom]] during a [[Harvest Feast|Feast Event]] (+5 )
- Active [[Crop Fever]] (+15 )
- Active [[Weather|Blooming]] (+5 ) (Could not be active during Jacob's Contest)
- Consuming [[Finn's Focaccia]] during a [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]] (+5 )
This brings the total to 185.8 Overbloom
#### Using a Farming Tool:
Any [[Farming Tools|4Mk. III Specialized Farming Tool]]
- Recombobulated and reforged with an [[Overpriced Drink]] (+7 )
- Enchanted with [[Feast|Feast V]] (+10 )
The maximum with an active [[Crop Fever]] is 202.8 Overbloom and 187.8 without.
- This is important to note because the player cannot efficiently harvest all crops using [[Sun's Grasp]], meaning this is the best for [[Cactus]], [[Cocoa Beans]], [[Melon|Melons]], and [[Pumpkin|Pumpkins]].
#### Using a Vacuum:
Taking into account the attribute [[Attributes|Pest Luck X]] ( [[Field Mouse Shard]]) (+5 ) grants against [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pest]], the new total maximum would go to 190.8 Overbloom and 185.2 without.
#### Using a [[Sun's Grasp]] (Actively farming with fist):
Considering this item doubles all Overbloom, the player would end with a grand total of: 371.6 Overbloom with an active [[Crop Fever]], and 341.6 without.
- To utilize [[Crop Fever]], the player would need to use a farming tool to activate the ability and then swap back to farming with a fist.
  - This can be a loss of hourly Rare Crop rates, making 348 Overbloom the more realistic and optimal [[Sun's Grasp]] maximum.
## Trivia
- Overbloom was first teased by 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]] motorgorilla in the Cowshed Discord server.
- An [[Ultimate Enchantments|Ultimate Enchantment]] that increased Overbloom during the day was teased by 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]] motorgorilla in Cowshed, which was later revealed to be Moonlit, and later renamed to [[Sunset]].

# Pet Luck
Aliases: Petluck
Categories: Player Statistics, Mechanics
[Pet Luck]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increasing the chance to drop Pets from killing mobs
Increasing: [[Taming]], [[Armor]], [[Weapons]], Reforges ([[Reforging]]), [[Enchantments]], [[Pets]], and More
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E013
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Player Skull ()
Pet Luck is a [[Stats|Stat]] that increases [[Magic Find]] for pet drops.
## Mechanics
### Dropping Pets from Mobs
Pet Luck increases the chance of all pets dropping from enemies with the following formula: Chance=BaseChance×(1+(MagicFind+PetLuck100))

# Pressure Resistance
Aliases: Pressure
Categories: Player Statistics
[Pressure Resistance]
Type: Stat
Uses: Reduces how fast you gain Pressure ([[Pressure Resistance]]) and postpones how deep you start gaining Pressure ([[Pressure Resistance]]).
Increasing: [[Heart of the Forest]], Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]), [[Scuba]], [[Abyssal Armor]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E01B
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Water Bottle ()
Pressure Resistance is one of the player's [[Stats|Misc Stats]]. It reduces how fast the player gains [[Pressure Resistance|Pressure]] when diving on The [[Moonglade Marsh]] or The [[Torrhus Canyon]], and also postpones how deep the player starts gaining [[Pressure Resistance|Pressure]].
## Mechanics
### Slowing [[Pressure Resistance|Pressure]] Gain
For every 1 Pressure Resistance the players will gain [[Pressure Resistance|Pressure]] 1% slower. At 100 Pressure Resistance, [[Pressure Resistance|Pressure]] will build up twice as slow.
### Postponing [[Pressure Resistance|Pressure]] Gain
Every 5 Pressure Resistance the player will start being affected by [[Pressure Resistance|Pressure]] 1 block deeper.
## Increasing Base Pressure Resistance

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Forest Essence]]|Extreme Pressure ([[Forest Essence]])|Essence Shop|Up to +30 Pressure Resistance|
|[[Mound of Seagrass]]|[[Mound of Seagrass]]|Consumable Item|+1 per consumption up to +5 Pressure Resistance|

### Maximizing Pressure Resistance
- [[Abyssal Armor]] (+80 )
- [[Enchantments|Scuba VI]] on all pieces of Armor (+24 )
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] or better [[Flying Fish Pet|Flying Fish Pet's]] Deep Sea Diver perk, which increases the stats of [[Diver's Armor]] and [[Abyssal Armor]] by up to 20%. (+20.8 when you wear [[Abyssal Armor]] + [[Enchantments|Scuba VI]] on all pieces of armor)
- [[Heart of the Forest|Deep Waters]] Heart of the Forest Perk (+50 )
- [[Attributes|Extreme Pressure]] Attribute ( [[Lumisquid Shard]]) (+10 )
- [[Attributes|Torrhus Diver]] Attribute ( [[Timil Shard]]) (+5 while on [[Torrhus Canyon]])

The maximum amount is 254.8 Pressure Resistance and 280.28 Pressure Resistance while [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry]] is elected.
## Tips
- Since players are only stopped from teleporting underwater after their [[Pressure Resistance|Pressure]] increases, increasing Pressure Resistance can help with underwater mobility.

# Pristine
Aliases: Prestine
Categories: Player Statistics, Temp/Using Skill Aliases
[Pristine]
Type: Stat
Uses: Chance to increase the quality of gemstones.
Increasing: [[Prismatic]], Topaz ([[Gemstone]]), [[Pets]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E01C
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Player Skull ()
Pristine is a [[Stats|stat]] that increases the chance to drop higher quality [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] from mining.
## Mechanics
- Every 1 Pristine grants a +1% chance to drop a Flawed [[Gemstone]] per Rough Gemstone initially yielded upon mining. They are also affected by [[Mining Fortune]].
- For example, if a player with x Pristine mines [[Gemstone|Ruby]], which drops 3–5 [[Gemstone|Rough Ruby Gemstones]], they will have an x% chance to roll a Flawed Gemstone 3-5 times. Then, [[Mining Fortune]] applies on both [[Gemstone|Rough]] and [[Gemstone|Flawed Ruby Gemstones]].
### Expected Drop Calculation
The expected drop converted into Rough gemstones can be calculated using the following formula:
- Average Rough Gemstones=Base Drop×(1+Pristine×79100)×(1+(Mining Fortune+Gemstone Fortune)×1100)
calcname  = Expected Rough Gemstones from a Gemstone Block
 calcpage  = Calculator:Gemstone drops
 template  = Template:Calculator/Gemstone drops
 form      = gemstoneDropsCalcForm
 result    = inner

 param     = base|Base Drop|4|number|0
 param     = pristine|Pristine|0|number|0
 param     = miningfortune|Mining Fortune|0|number|0
 param     = gemstonefortune|Gemstone Fortune|0|number|0
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
### Potency of Pristine versus Mining Fortunes
The gain of Rough Gemstones from increasing Pristine is not constant as it also depends on [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Gemstone Fortune]].
calcname  = Increase in Percent from gaining Pristine, Mining Fortune and Gemstone Fortune
 calcpage  = Calculator:Gemstone drops upgrades
 template  = Template:Calculator/Gemstone drops upgrades
 form      = gemstoneDropsUpgradesCalcForm
 result    = inner

 param     = currentPristine|Current Pristine|0|number|0
 param     = currentMiningFortune|Current Mining Fortune|0|number|0
 param     = currentGemstoneFortune|Current Gemstone Fortune|0|number|0
 param     = increasePristine|Increase in Pristine|0|number|0
 param     = increaseMiningFortune|Increase in Mining Fortune|0|number|0
 param     = increaseGemstoneFortune|Increase in Gemstone Fortune|0|number|0
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.

## Trivia
- Pristine is affected by both [[Catacombs]] levels and stars within [[Dungeons]]. However, this is useless as there are no [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] within Dungeons.
- Gemstones gained from Pristine do not give progress to Gemstone Collection [[Commissions]].

# Pull
Categories: Player Statistics
[Pull]
Type: Stat
Uses: Determines which fish you can pull with a Fishing Net ([[Fishing Nets]]).
Increasing: [[Fishing Nets]], Reforges ([[Reforging]]), [[Attributes]]
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E02D
Pull is one of the player's [[Stats|Hunting Stats]]. It determines what [[Mob|Mobs]] a player can pull with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]], as well as how long it takes to do so.
## Mechanics
The effects of Pull vary depending on which [[Mob|Mobs]] are being hunted.
|Mob|Effect|Required Net|
|-|-|-|
|[[Flipflopper]]|Damage taken by the mob from [[Fishing Nets]] are proportional to the player's Pull.|[[Medium Fishing Net]] or better|
|[[Joydive]]|(same as above)|(same as above)|
|[[Lumisquid]]|(same as above)|(same as above)|
|[[Seashine]]|(same as above)|(same as above)|
|[[Water Snake]]|(same as above)|[[Turbo Fishing Net]] or better|
|[[Shellwise]]|(same as above)||
|[[Salmon]]|The amount that a [[Salmon]] is slowed is proportional to the player's Pull.||
|[[Cod]]|The amount that a [[Cod]] spins is proportional to the player's Pull.||
## Increasing Pull
|Slot|Item||
|-|-|-|
|[[Basic Fishing Net]]|[[Basic Fishing Net]]|+5 Pull|
|[[Medium Fishing Net]]|[[Medium Fishing Net]]|+25 Pull|
|[[Turbo Fishing Net]]|[[Turbo Fishing Net]]|+50 Pull|
|[[Gigantic Fishing Net]]|[[Gigantic Fishing Net]]|+100 Pull|

|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
[Fishing Nets]
|[[Extremely Mild Adhesive]]|[[Extremely Mild Adhesive]] (Sticky)|+5|+7.5|+10|+15|+20|[[Miria]] 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Miria's Coupon]]|
Certain [[Attributes]] grant Pull.
|Attribute|Shard||
|-|-|-|
|Eelastic ([[Attributes]])|[[Eel Shard]]|+1 per level, up to +10|
## Maximum
The maximum achievable Pull is 130 .
- [[Gigantic Fishing Net]] - +100
  - [[Reforging|Reforged]] with [[Extremely Mild Adhesive|Sticky]] - +15
  - [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulated]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] - +5
- [[Attributes|Eelastic X]] - +10

# Respiration
Categories: Player Statistics, Confirmations needed
[Respiration]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the time you can spend underwater before taking drowning damage.
Increasing: Respiration ([[Respiration (Enchantment)]]), [[Snorkeling Armor]], [[Diver's Armor]], [[Abyssal Armor]], [[Respiration Talisman]] accessory line, and more
[Values]
Base Value: 30
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E01D
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Glass Bottle ()
Respiration is one of the player's [[Stats|Misc Stats]]. It determines how long a player can stay underwater before they start to drown and is affected by [[Pressure Resistance|Pressure]] and [[Pressure Resistance]].
## Mechanics
Respiration increases how long the player can stay underwater before starting to drown. For every 2 Respiration, the player can stay underwater 1 second longer before starting to drown.
The more [[Pressure Resistance|Pressure]] gathered, which is gained by going deeper underwater, the faster air bubbles are lost. Having more [[Pressure Resistance]] counters this.

#### Maximizing Respiration
- Base amount of 30 Respiration
- Donating all members of [[The Fish Family]] to [[Coral]] (+68 Respiration)
- [[Abyssal Armor]] (+20 Respiration)
  - [[Respiration (Enchantment)|Respiration IV]] (+60 Respiration)
  - [[Flying Fish Pet]] Deep Sea Diver ability (+16 Respiration)
    - [[Rubber Snorkel]] while on a [[Foraging]] Island (+10 Respiration)
- [[Attributes|Deep Diving]] attribute (+10 Respiration)
- [[Respiration Artifact]] (+15 Respiration)
- [[Isopod Husk]] (+5 Respiration)
- [[Heart of the Forest|Iron Lungs]] perk (+50 Respiration)
- [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry's]] Statpocalypse (+10%)
The maximum amount is 284 Respiration, [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] or 312.4 Respiration [Confirm] with [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry]].
## Tips
- Respiration and [[Pressure Resistance]] are two stats that are symbiotic, so it is best to increase both stats at the same time.

# Rift Damage
Categories: Player Statistics, Mechanics, Rift Dimension
[Rift Damage]
Type: Stat
Uses: Determines the Damage dealt in the Rift Dimension.
Increasing: [[Weapons]], and more
[Values]
Base Value: 20
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E01E
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Diamond Sword ()
Rift Damage is a [[Stats|Rift Stat]]. It determines how much damage the player deals in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Mechanics
The player has a base Rift Damage of 20, which can be increased through various items. The higher the player's Rift Damage, the more damage they deal to enemies in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Increasing Bonus Rift Damage
### Weapons
Certain [[Weapons]] grant Rift Damage. They must be used or held to grant their effects.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type|Rift Damage against non-vampires|Rift Damage against vampires|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wyld Sword]]|[[Wyld Sword]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Sword ([[Swords]])|+2|+2|
|[[Leech Sword]]|[[Leech Sword]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Sword ([[Swords]])|+3|+3|
|[[Tactician's Murder Weapon]]|[[Tactician's Murder Weapon]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Sword ([[Swords]])|+4|+4|
|[[Self-Recursive Pickaxe]]|[[Self-Recursive Pickaxe]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Sword ([[Swords]])|+5|+5|
|[[Silver-Laced Karambit]]|[[Silver-Laced Karambit]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Sword ([[Swords]])|+5|+8|
|[[Silver-Twist Karambit]]|[[Silver-Twist Karambit]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Sword ([[Swords]])|+6|+11|

### Maximizing Rift Damage
- Base Rift Damage (+20 )
- [[Silver-Twist Karambit]] (+6 )
- [[Leggings of the Coven]] or [[Wizardman Leggings]] (+6 maximum)
- [[Rift Necklace]] (+1 )
- [[Strength Elixir]] (+3 )
- [[Weirder Tuba]] (+2 when used)
This totals to 38 Rift Damage, and 55 Rift Damage when fighting Vampires with all [[Stillgore Château|Effigies]] active.
However, when hitting a Vampire less than 20% [[Health]] with a Steak Stake, it will total to 1050 Rift Damage.
